    if n and n >= 0 and n <= 2000 then
        _G._VioletteccSpinBotSpeed = n
        b.Text = tostring(n)
    else
        b.Text = tostring(_G._VioletteccSpinBotSpeed or 360)
    end
end)
_G._VioletteccRefreshSpinBotBoxes = function()
    if _G._VioletteccSpinBotSpeedBox  then _G._VioletteccSpinBotSpeedBox.Text  = tostring(_G._VioletteccSpinBotSpeed  or 360) end
    if _G._VioletteccSpinBotRadiusBox then _G._VioletteccSpinBotRadiusBox.Text = tostring(_G._VioletteccSpinBotRadius or 0) end
end

_G._VezyRotateLockOn    = false
_G._VezyRotateLockRange = 50
_G._VezyRotateLockConn  = nil

_G.VezyStartRotateLock = function() end

_G.VezyStopRotateLock = function()

        _G._VezyRotateLockConn:Disconnect()
        _G._VezyRotateLockConn = nil
    end
end

sRL = TR(pMovement, 58, "Rotate Lock", "", nil, false, function(on)
    _G._VezyRotateLockOn = on
    if on then _G.VezyStartRotateLock() else _G.VezyStopRotateLock() end
    if _G.KRIXSaveNow then _G.KRIXSaveNow() end
end); VisualSetters.RotateLock = sRL

task.defer(function()

    for _, child in ipairs(pMovement:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Frame") then
            for _, sub in ipairs(child:GetDescendants()) do
                if sub:IsA("TextLabel") and sub.Text == "Rotate Lock" then
                    child:Destroy()
                    return
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

do
    local mRow = Instance.new("Frame", pMovement)
    mRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, getMobileOptimized(38, 34))
    mRow.BackgroundColor3 = CARD
    mRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    mRow.LayoutOrder = 22
    mRow.ZIndex = 4
    Instance.new("UICorner", mRow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
    mRow.Visible = false
    table.insert(_aimRows, mRow)
    local mSt = Instance.new("UIStroke", mRow)
    mSt.Color = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 45); mSt.Thickness = 1; mSt.Transparency = 0.4

    local mLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", mRow)
    mLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 110, 1, 0)
    mLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
    mLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    mLbl.Text = "Aimbot Mode"
    mLbl.TextColor3 = WHITE
    mLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    mLbl.TextSize = getMobileOptimized(11, 10)
    mLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    mLbl.ZIndex = 5
    Instance.new("UIStroke", mLbl).Transparency = 1

    local mUnd = Instance.new("Frame", mLbl)
    mUnd.Size = UDim2.new(0, textWidth(mLbl.Text, mLbl.TextSize, mLbl.Font), 0, 1)
    mUnd.Visible = false
    mUnd.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 9)
    mUnd.BackgroundColor3 = ACC
    mUnd.BorderSizePixel = 0
    mUnd.ZIndex = 5

    local amBtns = {}
    -- { label, mode key, button width }
    local AM_OPTIONS = { { "Normal", "new", 52 }, { "Bypass", "old", 52 }, { "Bypass V2", "v3", 72 } }
    local MBH, MGAP = 24, 6
    local mTotal = MGAP * (#AM_OPTIONS - 1)
    for _, o in ipairs(AM_OPTIONS) do mTotal = mTotal + o[3] end
    local function aimModeLabel()
        local m = _G._VezyBatAimbotMode
        return (m == "old" and "Bypass") or (m == "v3" and "Bypass V2") or "Normal"
    end
    local function refreshAimMode()
        for k, b in pairs(amBtns) do
            local on = (k == aimModeLabel())
            b.BackgroundColor3 = on and ACC or Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30)
            b.TextColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(165, 165, 170)
        end
    end
    local amX = -(mTotal + 12)
    for _, opt in ipairs(AM_OPTIONS) do
        local v, modeKey, bw = opt[1], opt[2], opt[3]
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton", mRow)
        b.Size = UDim2.new(0, bw, 0, MBH)
        b.Position = UDim2.new(1, amX, 0.5, -MBH/2)
        amX = amX + bw + MGAP
        b.BackgroundColor3 = CARD2
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.AutoButtonColor = false
        b.Text = v
        b.TextColor3 = DIM
        b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        b.TextSize = getMobileOptimized(11, 10)
        b.ZIndex = 5
        Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        amBtns[v] = b
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            _G._VezyBatAimbotMode = modeKey
            refreshAimMode()
            if _G._VezyBatAimbotOn then
                if _G.VezyStopBatAimbot then pcall(_G.VezyStopBatAimbot) end
                task.wait(0.05)
                if _G.VezyStartBatAimbotDispatch then pcall(_G.VezyStartBatAimbotDispatch) end
            end
            if _G.KRIXSaveNowInstant then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNowInstant) end
            if _G.KRIXSaveNow then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNow) end
        end)
    end
    _G._VynxRefreshAimbotModeBtns = refreshAimMode
    refreshAimMode()
end

            rlInput.Text = tostring(_G._VezyRotateLockRange or 50)
        end
        if _G.KRIXSaveNow then _G.KRIXSaveNow() end

sectionHeader(pSteal, "STEAL CONFIGURATION", 31)

_G._VynxStealRadiusNormal = tonumber(_G._VynxStealRadiusNormal) or tonumber(Settings.StealRadius) or 62
_G._VynxStealRadiusSemi   = tonumber(_G._VynxStealRadiusSemi)   or 8.7
_G._VynxAutoGrabPause = tonumber(_G._VynxAutoGrabPause) or 75

_G._VynxNormalizeStealMode = function()
    local mode = tostring(_G._VynxAutoStealMode or "Normal")
    -- Semi removed — always Normal
    if mode == "75" or mode == "80" or mode == "85" or mode == "90" then
        _G._VynxAutoGrabPause = tonumber(mode)
    end
    mode = "Normal"
    _G._VynxAutoStealMode = mode
    local pause = tonumber(_G._VynxAutoGrabPause) or 75
    pause = math.clamp(math.floor(pause + 0.5), 73, 90)
    _G._VynxAutoGrabPause = pause
    return mode
end
_G._VynxNormalizeStealMode()

local function _curStealMode()
    return "Normal"
end
local function _curStealRadius()
    return _G._VynxStealRadiusNormal or tonumber(Settings.StealRadius) or 62
end
local function _applyStealRadius(n)
    _G._VynxStealRadiusNormal = n
    Settings.StealRadius = n; Values.STEAL_RADIUS = n
    if _G.CandyNormalAutoStealSetRadius then pcall(_G.CandyNormalAutoStealSetRadius, n) end
end
RadiusInput = NR(pSteal, 34, "Radius", _curStealRadius(), function(v,b)
    local n = tonumber(v)
    if n then
        n = math.clamp(n,0.5,500); n = math.floor(n*10+0.5)/10
        _applyStealRadius(n)
        b.Text = tostring(n); cachedPrompts = {}; promptCacheTime = 0
        if _G.KRIXSaveNow then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNow) end
    else b.Text = tostring(_curStealRadius()) end
end)
_G._VynxStealDelayRadius = tonumber(_G._VynxStealDelayRadius) or 9
-- Delay Radius: Auto Steal pauses at the Grab Pause %; while you are inside this
-- many studs of the brainrot it continues on to 100%.
_G._VynxDelayRadiusBox = NR(pSteal, 35, "Delay Radius", _G._VynxStealDelayRadius, function(v, b)
    local n = tonumber(v)
    if n then
        n = math.clamp(n, 0.5, 500); n = math.floor(n * 10 + 0.5) / 10
        _G._VynxStealDelayRadius = n
        _G.K7NormalSteal = _G.K7NormalSteal or {}
        _G.K7NormalSteal.delayRadius = n
        b.Text = tostring(n)
        if _G.KRIXSaveNow then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNow) end
    else
        b.Text = tostring(_G._VynxStealDelayRadius or 9)
    end
end)

    if _G._VynxDelayRadiusBox then _G._VynxDelayRadiusBox.Text = tostring(tonumber(_G._VynxStealDelayRadius) or 9) end
end

do
    local f = makeRowBase(pSteal, 32)
    f.Name = "StealV1Row"
    nameWithUnderline(f, "STEAL V1")
    local setV = mkSwitch(f, Enabled.AutoSteal)
    _G._KRIXAutoStealSet = function(on) setV(on) end
    VisualSetters.AutoSteal = function(on) setV(on == true) end
    _G._VynxRefreshAutoSteal = function() setV(Enabled.AutoSteal == true) end
    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", f)
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0); btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    btn.Text = ""; btn.ZIndex = 8
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Enabled.AutoSteal = not Enabled.AutoSteal
        Settings.AutoStealEnabled = Enabled.AutoSteal
        setV(Enabled.AutoSteal)
        if Enabled.AutoSteal then
            startAutoSteal()
        else
            stopAutoSteal()
        end
        if _G.KRIXSaveNow then _G.KRIXSaveNow() end
    end)

    _G._VynxNormalizeStealMode()
    -- Steal Mode dropdown removed (Semi disabled; Normal only)
    local _setModeDrop, _modePanel = nil, nil

    local function _applyGrabPausePct(raw)
        local pause = tonumber((tostring(raw or ""):gsub("%%", ""))) or tonumber(_G._VynxAutoGrabPause) or 75
        pause = math.clamp(math.floor(pause + 0.5), 73, 90)
        _G._VynxAutoGrabPause = pause
        local dur = tonumber(STEAL_DURATION_FIXED) or 1.30
        _G.K7NormalSteal = _G.K7NormalSteal or {}
        _G.K7NormalSteal.stopTime = dur * (pause / 100)
        _G.K7NormalSteal.stopEnabled = true
        return pause
    end

    local _pauseRow = makeRowBase(pSteal, 34)
    _pauseRow.Name = "AutoGrabPauseRow"
    nameWithUnderline(_pauseRow, "Grab Pause")

    local _pauseCustomTb
    local function _syncPauseCustomBox()
        if _pauseCustomTb then
            _pauseCustomTb.Text = tostring(tonumber(_G._VynxAutoGrabPause) or 75)
        end
    end

    local _setPauseDrop, _pausePanel = makeDropdown(_pauseRow, {"75%", "80%", "85%"},
        function()
            local p = tonumber(_G._VynxAutoGrabPause) or 75
            if p == 75 or p == 80 or p == 85 then return tostring(p) .. "%" end
            return tostring(p) .. "%"
        end,
        function(v)
            local pause = _applyGrabPausePct(v)
            _syncPauseCustomBox()
            if _setPauseDrop then pcall(_setPauseDrop) end
            if Enabled.AutoSteal then pcall(startAutoSteal) end
            if _G.KRIXSaveNow then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNow) end
        end, 58, SW_W + 20, "Grab Pause")

    -- Typeable % box placed on the dropdown panel to the right of the 85% chip
    if _pausePanel then
        local customBx = Instance.new("Frame", _pausePanel)
        customBx.Name = "GrabPauseCustom"
        customBx.Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 24)
        -- Options are right-aligned: 85% is the rightmost chip; put box just to its right edge inset
        customBx.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12 - 44, 0.5, -12)
        customBx.BackgroundColor3 = BG_DARK
        customBx.BorderSizePixel = 0
        customBx.ZIndex = 6
        Instance.new("UICorner", customBx).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        local customSt = Instance.new("UIStroke", customBx)
        customSt.Color = ACC
        customSt.Thickness = 1
        customSt.Transparency = 0.45
        customSt.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        _pauseCustomTb = Instance.new("TextBox", customBx)
        _pauseCustomTb.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        _pauseCustomTb.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        _pauseCustomTb.Text = tostring(tonumber(_G._VynxAutoGrabPause) or 75)
        _pauseCustomTb.TextColor3 = WHITE
        _pauseCustomTb.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        _pauseCustomTb.TextSize = getMobileOptimized(11, 10)
        _pauseCustomTb.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        _pauseCustomTb.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        _pauseCustomTb.PlaceholderText = "73-90"
        _pauseCustomTb.PlaceholderColor3 = DIM
        _pauseCustomTb.ZIndex = 7
        _pauseCustomTb.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            local pause = _applyGrabPausePct(_pauseCustomTb.Text)
            _pauseCustomTb.Text = tostring(pause)
            if _setPauseDrop then pcall(_setPauseDrop) end
            if Enabled.AutoSteal then pcall(startAutoSteal) end
            if _G.KRIXSaveNow then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNow) end
        end)

        -- Shift the 75/80/85 chips left to make room for the custom box
        task.defer(function()
            for _, ch in ipairs(_pausePanel:GetChildren()) do
                if ch:IsA("TextButton") and (ch.Text == "75%" or ch.Text == "80%" or ch.Text == "85%") then
                    local p = ch.Position
                    ch.Position = UDim2.new(p.X.Scale, p.X.Offset - 50, p.Y.Scale, p.Y.Offset)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local function _syncPauseRow()
        local isNormal = (_curStealMode() == "Normal")
        if _pauseRow then _pauseRow.Visible = isNormal end
        if _pausePanel and not isNormal then _pausePanel.Visible = false end
    end

    _G._VynxRefreshAutoGrabPause = function()
        _G._VynxNormalizeStealMode()
        local pause = _applyGrabPausePct(_G._VynxAutoGrabPause)
        _syncPauseCustomBox()
        if _setPauseDrop then pcall(_setPauseDrop) end
        _syncPauseRow()
    end

    _G._VynxRefreshAutoStealMode = function()
        _G._VynxNormalizeStealMode()
        if _setModeDrop then pcall(_setModeDrop) end
        if _G._VynxRefreshStealRadius then pcall(_G._VynxRefreshStealRadius) end
        if _G._VynxRefreshAutoGrabPause then pcall(_G._VynxRefreshAutoGrabPause) end
    end
    _G._VynxRefreshAutoGrabPause()
end

local BG_OPTIONS = {
    {key = "off",   label = "None",   id = ""},
    -- The former seventh background is now the first selectable one.
    {key = "bg7",   label = "BG 1",   id = "71792293290595"},
    {key = "bg4",   label = "BG 4",   id = "125423918880160"},
    {key = "bg6",   label = "BG 6",   id = "119934726907659"},
    {key = "bg8",   label = "BG 8",   id = "121229055542067"},
}

local BG_ACCENTS = {
    off  = Color3.fromRGB(175, 95, 255),
    bg1  = Color3.fromRGB(175, 95, 255),
    bg2  = Color3.fromRGB(175, 95, 255),
    bg3  = Color3.fromRGB(175, 95, 255),
    bg4  = Color3.fromRGB(175, 95, 255),
    bg5  = Color3.fromRGB(175, 95, 255),
    bg6  = Color3.fromRGB(175, 95, 255),
    bg7  = Color3.fromRGB(175, 95, 255),
    bg8  = Color3.fromRGB(175, 95, 255),
    bg9  = Color3.fromRGB(175, 95, 255),
    bg10 = Color3.fromRGB(175, 95, 255),
}

_G._VioletteccAccent = ACC

-- ===== Theme color mapping =====
local ThemeMap = (function()
    local M = {}
    M.PURPLE = Color3.fromRGB(175, 95, 255)
    local TP_H, TP_S, TP_V = M.PURPLE:ToHSV()
    M.reg = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
    M.scanned = false
    -- Images/logos have their own tint logic and must not be hue-shifted here
    M.SKIP = {
        VioletteHeaderLogo = true, VioletteHeaderLogoGlow = true,
        VioletteHeaderLogoBoost = true, VioletteTitleImage = true,
        BackgroundImage = true,
    }

    function M.isPurpleish(c)
        local h, s, v = c:ToHSV()
        return h >= 0.68 and h <= 0.86 and s >= 0.2 and v >= 0.25
    end

    -- Moves a purple shade to the theme hue, keeping its lightness/saturation feel
    function M.target(orig, theme)
        if orig == M.PURPLE then return theme end
        local h, s, v = orig:ToHSV()
        local th, ts, tv = theme:ToHSV()
        return Color3.fromHSV((h - TP_H + th) % 1,
            math.clamp(s * ts / TP_S, 0, 1),
            math.clamp(v * tv / TP_V, 0, 1))
    end

    local function seqEqual(seq, kps)
        local a = seq.Keypoints
        if #a ~= #kps then return false end
        for i, kp in ipairs(a) do
            if kp.Value ~= kps[i].Value then return false end
        end
        return true
    end

    function M.repaintGradient(g, old, theme, canRegister)
        local entry = M.reg[g]
        if not entry then
            local has = false
            for _, kp in ipairs(g.Color.Keypoints) do
                if kp.Value == old or (canRegister and M.isPurpleish(kp.Value)) then has = true break end
            end
            if not has then return end
            local origKps = {}
            for i, kp in ipairs(g.Color.Keypoints) do
                origKps[i] = {Time = kp.Time, Value = (kp.Value == old) and M.PURPLE or kp.Value}
            end
            entry = {orig = origKps, last = g.Color.Keypoints}
            M.reg[g] = entry
        elseif not seqEqual(g.Color, entry.last) then
            return
        end
        local out = {}
        for i, kp in ipairs(entry.orig) do
            local v = kp.Value
            if v == M.PURPLE or M.isPurpleish(v) then v = M.target(v, theme) end
            out[i] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(kp.Time, v)
        end
        g.Color = ColorSequence.new(out)
        entry.last = g.Color.Keypoints
    end
    return M
end)()

local function applyAccent(newColor)
    if typeof(newColor) ~= "Color3" then return end
    local old = _G._VioletteccAccent or ACC
    if newColor == old then return end

    -- Every purple-toned color in the menu (accent, light text, dark panels)
    -- is hue-shifted to the chosen theme, not just exact accent matches.
    local firstPass = not ThemeMap.scanned
    ThemeMap.scanned = true
    local oldIsPurple = (old == ThemeMap.PURPLE)

    local function repaint(inst, prop)
        local ok, cur = pcall(function() return inst[prop] end)
        if not ok or typeof(cur) ~= "Color3" then return end
        local reg = ThemeMap.reg[inst]
        if not reg then reg = {}; ThemeMap.reg[inst] = reg end
        local entry = reg[prop]
        if not entry then
            if cur == old then
                entry = {orig = ThemeMap.PURPLE, last = cur}
            elseif firstPass and oldIsPurple and ThemeMap.isPurpleish(cur) then
                entry = {orig = cur, last = cur}
            else
                return
            end
            reg[prop] = entry
        elseif cur ~= entry.last and cur ~= old then
            return -- changed by other code (e.g. a toggle going gray); leave it
        end
        pcall(function()
            inst[prop] = ThemeMap.target(entry.orig, newColor)
            entry.last = inst[prop]
        end)
    end

    for _, d in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
        pcall(function()
            if d:IsA("UIStroke") then
                repaint(d, "Color")
            elseif d:IsA("UIGradient") then
                ThemeMap.repaintGradient(d, old, newColor, firstPass and oldIsPurple)
            elseif d:IsA("GuiObject") then
                if not ThemeMap.SKIP[d.Name] then
                    repaint(d, "BackgroundColor3")
                    repaint(d, "BorderColor3")
                    if d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox") then
                        repaint(d, "TextColor3")
                    end
                    if d:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
                        repaint(d, "ScrollBarImageColor3")
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    ACC = newColor
    _G._VioletteccAccent = newColor
    -- Header logo + reopen-button logo follow the selected theme color
    local logoTween = TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    local function tintLogo(img)
        if img and img.Parent then
            img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            _G._VioletteccApplyLogoTheme(img, newColor, false)
        end
    end
    for _, letter in ipairs(hdrWordmark) do tintLogo(letter) end
    tintLogo(gui:FindFirstChild("VioletteTitleImage", true))
    if _G._VezyRefreshBgTint then pcall(_G._VezyRefreshBgTint, newColor) end
    if _G._VynxRefreshMobileBtnColors then pcall(_G._VynxRefreshMobileBtnColors) end
    if _G._VioletteccRepaintTabs then pcall(_G._VioletteccRepaintTabs) end
    if _G._VioletteccSetTagColor then pcall(_G._VioletteccSetTagColor, newColor) end
    if _G._VioletteccRefreshAvatarESPColor then pcall(_G._VioletteccRefreshAvatarESPColor) end
    if _G._VioletteccRefreshRagdollCDColor then pcall(_G._VioletteccRefreshRagdollCDColor) end
    if _G._AdaptRefreshESPColor then pcall(_G._AdaptRefreshESPColor, newColor) end
    if _G._VioletteccRefreshPanels then pcall(_G._VioletteccRefreshPanels) end
end

_G._VioletteccApplyAccent = applyAccent
-- Maps any original purple shade to the active theme (used by buttons, billboards)
_G._VioletteccThemeColor = function(c)
    return ThemeMap.target(c, _G._VioletteccAccent or ACC)
end
_G._VioletteccAccentForBg = function(key)
    return BG_ACCENTS[key or "off"] or BG_ACCENTS.off
end

-- Theme palette (Purple is the original Violette accent)
local THEME_COLORS = {
    Purple = Color3.fromRGB(222, 190, 130), -- displayed as ANGEL; kept as Purple internally for config compatibility
    Red    = Color3.fromRGB(255, 96, 112),
    Blue   = Color3.fromRGB(55, 145, 255),
    Pink   = Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 185),
}
local THEME_ORDER = { "Purple", "Red", "Blue", "Pink" }
_G._VioletteccThemeName = _G._VioletteccThemeName or "Purple"

do
    local themeRow = Instance.new("Frame", pSettings)
    themeRow.Name = "ThemeSwitcher"
    themeRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, getMobileOptimized(56, 50))
    themeRow.BackgroundColor3 = CARD
    themeRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    themeRow.LayoutOrder = 3
    themeRow.ZIndex = 4
    Instance.new("UICorner", themeRow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
    local tStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", themeRow)
    tStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(40, 44, 56)
    tStroke.Thickness = 1
    tStroke.Transparency = 0.5

    local themeTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel", themeRow)
    themeTitle.Size = UDim2.new(0.35, 0, 1, 0)
    themeTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
    themeTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    themeTitle.Text = "Theme"
    themeTitle.TextColor3 = WHITE
    themeTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    themeTitle.TextSize = getMobileOptimized(13, 12)
    themeTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    themeTitle.ZIndex = 5

    local chipHold = Instance.new("Frame", themeRow)
    chipHold.Size = UDim2.new(0.65, -16, 0, 28)
    chipHold.Position = UDim2.new(0.35, 0, 0.5, -14)
    chipHold.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    chipHold.ZIndex = 5
    local chipLay = Instance.new("UIListLayout", chipHold)
    chipLay.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    chipLay.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
    chipLay.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
    chipLay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    chipLay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

    local themeChips = {}
    local function refreshThemeChips()
        local cur = tostring(_G._VioletteccThemeName or "Purple")
        for name, btn in pairs(themeChips) do
            local on = (name == cur)
            local col = THEME_COLORS[name]
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = on and col or Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 34)
            btn.TextColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(12, 10, 16) or Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 190)
            local st = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
            if st and col then
                st.Color = col
                st.Transparency = on and 0.1 or 0.45
            end
        end
    end

    local function setTheme(name)
        name = THEME_COLORS[name] and name or "Purple"
        _G._VioletteccThemeName = name
        local col = THEME_COLORS[name]
        if col and _G._VioletteccApplyAccent then
            pcall(_G._VioletteccApplyAccent, col)
        elseif col then
            applyAccent(col)
        end
        refreshThemeChips()
        if _G.KRIXSaveNow then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNow) end
    end

    for i, name in ipairs(THEME_ORDER) do
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton", chipHold)
        b.Size = UDim2.new(0, getMobileOptimized(52, 46), 0, 26)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 34)
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.AutoButtonColor = false
        b.Text = (name == "Purple") and "ANGEL" or name:upper()
        b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        b.TextSize = getMobileOptimized(11, 10)
        b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 190)
        b.LayoutOrder = i
        b.ZIndex = 6
        Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", b)
        st.Color = THEME_COLORS[name]
        st.Thickness = 1
        st.Transparency = 0.45
        st.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            setTheme(name)
        end)
        themeChips[name] = b
    end

    _G._VioletteccSetTheme = setTheme
    _G._VioletteccRefreshThemeChips = refreshThemeChips
    task.defer(function()
        -- Apply the Angel palette by default; a saved user theme may override it later.
        local cur = tostring(_G._VioletteccThemeName or "Purple")
        if not THEME_COLORS[cur] then
            _G._VioletteccThemeName = "Purple"
            cur = "Purple"
        end
        setTheme(cur)
        refreshThemeChips()
    end)
end

        local step = (CW + CGAP) * 2
        local maxX = math.max(0, strip.AbsoluteCanvasSize.X - strip.AbsoluteSize.X)
        local target = math.clamp(strip.CanvasPosition.X + dir * step, 0, maxX)
        TweenService:Create(strip, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
            CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(target, 0)
        }):Play()
    end

    end

    local function setActiveBtn(activeKey)
        local accent = _G._VioletteccAccent or ACC
        for k, btn in pairs(bgPickerBtns) do
            local isActive = (k == activeKey)

                s.Color = isActive and accent or Color3.fromRGB(50, 52, 60)
                TweenService:Create(s, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                    Thickness = isActive and 2 or 1,
                    Transparency = isActive and 0 or 0.4,
                }):Play()
            end
            local tick = btn:FindFirstChild("Tick")
            if tick then
                tick.Visible = isActive
                tick.TextColor3 = accent
            end
            TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {

        tick.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
        tick.Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 2)
        tick.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        tick.Text = utf8.char(0x2713)
        tick.TextColor3 = ACC
        tick.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        tick.TextSize = 13
        tick.Visible = false
        tick.ZIndex = 8

        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            _G.VezySetBgImage(opt.id)
            -- Keep the selected Theme color; backgrounds no longer force purple
            setActiveBtn(opt.key)
            if _G.KRIXSaveNow then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNow) end
        end)
        bgPickerBtns[opt.key] = b
    end

    -- Re-tint the menu background and the picker previews to the active theme
    _G._VezyRefreshBgTint = function(accent)
        accent = accent or _G._VioletteccAccent or ACC
        local tint = _G._VezyBgTintFor(accent)
        local info = TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        if _G._VezyBgImageRef and _G._VezyBgImageRef.Parent then
            TweenService:Create(_G._VezyBgImageRef, info, {ImageColor3 = tint}):Play()
        end
        for _, btn in pairs(bgPickerBtns) do
            if btn and btn.Parent then
                TweenService:Create(btn, info, {ImageColor3 = tint}):Play()
            end
        end
    end

    task.defer(function()
        setActiveBtn(getCurrentKey())
    end)
    setActiveBtn(getCurrentKey())

    VisualSetters.BgImage = function(on)

        if type(on) == "boolean" then
            _G.VezySetBgImage(on and BG_OPTIONS[2].id or "")
            setActiveBtn(on and "bg7" or "off")
        end
    end

    _G.VezySyncBgPicker = function()
        setActiveBtn(getCurrentKey())
        if _G._VioletteccRefreshThemeChips then pcall(_G._VioletteccRefreshThemeChips) end
    end
end

    local function makePresets(row, values, getV, setV)
        local btns = {}
        local BW, BH, GAP = 42, 24, 6
        local total = #values * BW + (#values - 1) * GAP
        local function refresh()
            for v, b in pairs(btns) do
                local on = (v == getV())
                TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                    BackgroundColor3 = on and ACC or Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30),
                    TextColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(165, 165, 170),
                }):Play()
            end
        end
        for i, v in ipairs(values) do
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
            b.Size = UDim2.new(0, BW, 0, BH)
            b.Position = UDim2.new(1, -(total + 12) + (i - 1) * (BW + GAP), 0.5, -BH/2)
            b.BackgroundColor3 = CARD2
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.AutoButtonColor = false
            b.Text = tostring(v)
            b.TextColor3 = DIM
            b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            b.TextSize = getMobileOptimized(11, 10)
            b.ZIndex = 5
            Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
            btns[v] = b
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                setV(v)
                refresh()
                if _G.KRIXSaveNowInstant then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNowInstant) end
                if _G.KRIXSaveNow then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNow) end
            end)
        end
        refresh()
        return refresh
    end

    local FOV_MIN, FOV_MAX = 70, 120
    local function clampFov(v)
        return math.clamp(math.floor((tonumber(v) or FOV_MAX) + 0.5), FOV_MIN, FOV_MAX)
    end

    local fovValRow = mkValueRow(23, "Normal FOV")
    _G._VezyCustomFOV = clampFov(_G._VezyCustomFOV)
    _G._VezyRefreshCustomFOV = makeStepper(fovValRow,
        function() return clampFov(_G._VezyCustomFOV) end,
        function(v)
            v = clampFov(v)
            if _G.VezySetCustomFOV then _G.VezySetCustomFOV(v) else _G._VezyCustomFOV = v end
            if _G.KRIXSaveNowInstant then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNowInstant) end
            if _G.KRIXSaveNow then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNow) end
        end, FOV_MIN, FOV_MAX, 10, "%d")

    local STRETCH_MIN, STRETCH_MAX, STRETCH_DEFAULT = 0.3, 1.0, 0.70
    local function clampStretch(v)
        v = tonumber(v)
        if not v then return STRETCH_DEFAULT end
        return math.clamp(v, STRETCH_MIN, STRETCH_MAX)
    end
    _G._VynxStretchValue = clampStretch(_G._VynxStretchValue or STRETCH_DEFAULT)
    local stretchValRow
    _G._VezyRefreshStretchFOV, stretchValRow = makeSliderRow(pVisual, 24, "Stretch Rez",
        STRETCH_MIN, STRETCH_MAX,
        function() return clampStretch(_G._VynxStretchValue) end,
        function(v)
            _G._VynxStretchValue = clampStretch(v)
            if _G.KRIXSaveNowInstant then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNowInstant) end
            if _G.KRIXSaveNow then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNow) end
        end, 0.05)
    stretchValRow.Visible = false

    local function refreshMode()
        local mode = _G._VezyDisplayMode
        for k, b in pairs(modeBtns) do
            local on = (k == mode)
            TweenService:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                BackgroundColor3 = on and ACC or Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30),
                TextColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(165, 165, 170),
            }):Play()
        end
        fovValRow.Visible     = (mode == "FOV")
        stretchValRow.Visible = (mode == "STRETCH")
    end

    local function applyMode(mode)
        _G._VezyDisplayMode = mode
        stretchRezEnabled = (mode == "STRETCH")
        if mode == "STRETCH" then
            if _G._VynxEnableStretchRez then pcall(_G._VynxEnableStretchRez) end
        else
            if _G._VynxDisableStretchRez then pcall(_G._VynxDisableStretchRez) end
        end
        if mode == "FOV" then
            if _G.VezyEnableFOV then pcall(_G.VezyEnableFOV) end
        else
            if _G.VezyDisableFOV then pcall(_G.VezyDisableFOV) end
        end
        refreshMode()
        if _G.KRIXSaveNowInstant then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNowInstant) end
        if _G.KRIXSaveNow then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNow) end
    end

    bDefault.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() applyMode("DEFAULT") end)
    bFov.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()     applyMode("FOV")     end)
    bStretch.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() applyMode("STRETCH") end)

    VisualSetters.StretchRez = function(on)
        applyMode(on and "STRETCH" or "DEFAULT")
    end
    VisualSetters.CustomFOV = function(on)
        applyMode(on and "FOV" or "DEFAULT")
    end
    _G._VezyRefreshDisplayMode = refreshMode

    refreshMode()
end

    noCamCollisionEnabled = on
    if on then enableNoCameraCollision() else disableNoCameraCollision() end

end); VisualSetters.NoCam = sNC

sectionHeader(pVisual, "ESP CONFIGURATION", 27)

_G._VynxAuraESP        = _G._VynxAuraESP or false
_G._VynxBoxedESP       = _G._VynxBoxedESP or false
_G._VynxShowTracker    = _G._VynxShowTracker or false
_G._VynxAvatarESP      = _G._VynxAvatarESP or false
_G._VynxRagdollCountdown = _G._VynxRagdollCountdown or false

local ESP_KINDS = { "Aura", "Box", "Tracer", "Avatar", "Timer" }

local ESP_BINDINGS = {
    ["Aura"]   = { key = "_VynxAuraESP",          setter = "AuraESP" },
    ["Box"]    = { key = "_VynxBoxedESP",         setter = "BoxedESP" },
    ["Tracer"] = { key = "_VynxShowTracker",      setter = "ShowTracker" },
    ["Avatar"] = { key = "_VynxAvatarESP",        setter = "AvatarESP" },
    ["Timer"]  = { key = "_VynxRagdollCountdown", setter = "RagdollCountdown" },
}

local function espKindOn(kind)
    local b = ESP_BINDINGS[kind]
    return b ~= nil and _G[b.key] == true
end

local function setESPKind(kind, on)
    local b = ESP_BINDINGS[kind]
    if not b then return end
    _G[b.key] = on and true or false
    if kind == "Avatar" and _G._VioletteccSetAvatarESP then
        pcall(_G._VioletteccSetAvatarESP, _G[b.key])
    end
    if kind == "Aura" and _G._AdaptESPSetEnabled then
        pcall(_G._AdaptESPSetEnabled, _G[b.key])
    end
    if VisualSetters[b.setter] then pcall(VisualSetters[b.setter], _G[b.key]) end
    if _G.KRIXSaveNow then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNow) end
end

local function anyESPOn()
    for _, kind in ipairs(ESP_KINDS) do
        if espKindOn(kind) then return true end
    end
    return false
end

local espSel = {}

local function anySelected()
    for _, kind in ipairs(ESP_KINDS) do
        if espSel[kind] then return true end
    end
    return false
end

local function espSelSave()
    local list = {}
    for _, kind in ipairs(ESP_KINDS) do
        if espSel[kind] then list[#list + 1] = kind end
    end
    _G._VynxESPSelection = table.concat(list, ",")
end

local function espSelLoad()
    for _, kind in ipairs(ESP_KINDS) do espSel[kind] = nil end
    local raw = _G._VynxESPSelection
    if type(raw) == "string" and raw ~= "" then
        for kind in string.gmatch(raw, "[^,]+") do
            if ESP_BINDINGS[kind] then espSel[kind] = true end
        end
    elseif type(raw) ~= "string" then
        for _, kind in ipairs(ESP_KINDS) do
            if espKindOn(kind) then espSel[kind] = true end
        end
        espSelSave()
    end
end
espSelLoad()
if _G._VynxESPOn == nil then _G._VynxESPOn = anyESPOn() end

local sESP, _espRow
local refreshESPOptions = function() end

local applyingESP = false

local function applyESP()
    applyingESP = true
    for _, kind in ipairs(ESP_KINDS) do
        setESPKind(kind, _G._VynxESPOn and espSel[kind] or false)
    end
    applyingESP = false
    espSelSave()
    refreshESPOptions()
end

sESP, _espRow = TR(pVisual, 28, "ESP", "", nil, _G._VynxESPOn, function(on)
    _G._VynxESPOn = on and true or false
    if _G._VynxESPOn and not anySelected() then espSel["Aura"] = true end
    applyESP()
    if _G.KRIXSaveNowInstant then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNowInstant) end
    if _G.KRIXSaveNow then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNow) end
end)
VisualSetters.ESPMain = sESP

_G._VynxRefreshESPKind = makeDropdown(_espRow, ESP_KINDS,
    function(kind) return espSel[kind] == true end,
    function(kind)
        espSel[kind] = not espSel[kind] or nil
        if espSel[kind] and not _G._VynxESPOn then
            _G._VynxESPOn = true
            pcall(sESP, true)
        end
        applyESP()
        if _G.KRIXSaveNowInstant then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNowInstant) end
        if _G.KRIXSaveNow then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNow) end
    end, 52, SW_W + 20, "ESP Type", true)
refreshESPOptions = _G._VynxRefreshESPKind

for _, kind in ipairs(ESP_KINDS) do
    local b = ESP_BINDINGS[kind]
    VisualSetters[b.setter] = function(on)
        if applyingESP then return end
        if on ~= nil then
            espSel[kind] = on and true or nil
            if on then _G._VynxESPOn = true end
            espSelSave()
        end
        refreshESPOptions()
        pcall(sESP, _G._VynxESPOn)
    end
end

_G._VynxSyncESPRow = function()
    espSelLoad()
    if _G._VynxESPOn == nil then _G._VynxESPOn = anyESPOn() end
    applyESP()
    pcall(sESP, _G._VynxESPOn)
end
end

-- The Panels category and its three launch buttons were intentionally removed
-- from Settings. The panels' existing runtime code is left untouched.

-- ══ Anti Bat (opened from Settings > Anti Bat button) ══
pcall(function()
_G._VioletteccAntiBatUI = (function()
--[[
	violette anti bat
	Clean anti-bat utility styled for Violette.

	Features:
	- Anti Bat (velocity desync)
	- Infinite Jump
	- Draggable GUI with toggles + keybind
	- Background picker (Violette hub backgrounds)
	- Config save/load (violette_antiBat_config.json)
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local panelOpen = false   -- window visibility (anti bat itself keeps running when hidden)
local _conns = {}

-- Violette hub backgrounds (same set as Violette duels / hub visual picker)
local BG_OPTIONS = {
	{ key = "off", label = "None", id = "" },
	{ key = "bg1", label = "BG 1", id = "71792293290595" },
	{ key = "bg4", label = "BG 4", id = "125423918880160" },
	{ key = "bg6", label = "BG 6", id = "119934726907659" },
	{ key = "bg8", label = "BG 8", id = "121229055542067" },
}

local config = {
	AntiBatEnabled = false,
	InfJumpEnabled = false,
	Keybind = "V",
	BackgroundId = "71792293290595",
}

local function saveConfig()
	if writefile then
		pcall(function()
			writefile("violette_antiBat_config.json", HttpService:JSONEncode(config))
		end)
	end
end

local function loadConfig()
	if isfile and isfile("violette_antiBat_config.json") then
		local ok, data = pcall(function()
			return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile("violette_antiBat_config.json"))
		end)
		if ok and type(data) == "table" then
			if type(data.Keybind) == "string" then config.Keybind = data.Keybind end
			if type(data.AntiBatEnabled) == "boolean" then config.AntiBatEnabled = data.AntiBatEnabled end
			if type(data.InfJumpEnabled) == "boolean" then config.InfJumpEnabled = data.InfJumpEnabled end
			if type(data.BackgroundId) == "string" then config.BackgroundId = data.BackgroundId end
		end
	end
end
loadConfig()

-- Anti Bat (velocity desync)
local antiBatConn = nil
local antiBatActive = false

local function enableAntiBat()
	if antiBatConn then return end
	antiBatActive = true
	antiBatConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if not antiBatActive then return end
		local char = LocalPlayer.Character
		if not char then return end
		local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not hrp then return end
		local vel = hrp.Velocity
		local y = vel.Y
		hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(math.random(-4000, 4000), y, math.random(-4000, 4000))
		RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
		if hrp then
			hrp.Velocity = vel
		end
	end)
end

local function disableAntiBat()
	antiBatActive = false
	if antiBatConn then
		antiBatConn:Disconnect()
		antiBatConn = nil
	end
end

-- Infinite Jump
local infJumpConn = nil
local infJumpActive = false
local forceJump = false

local function enableInfJump()
	if infJumpConn then return end
	infJumpActive = true
	infJumpConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if not infJumpActive then return end
		local char = LocalPlayer.Character
		if not char then return end
		local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not humanoid or not hrp then return end
		if humanoid.Jump or forceJump then
			local vel = hrp.Velocity
			if vel.Y < 50 then
				hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(vel.X, 50, vel.Z)
			end
		end
	end)
end

local function disableInfJump()
	infJumpActive = false
	if infJumpConn then
		infJumpConn:Disconnect()
		infJumpConn = nil
	end
end

local function onJumping()
	if infJumpActive then
		forceJump = true
		task.wait(0.1)
		forceJump = false
	end
end

_conns[#_conns + 1] = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
	task.wait(0.5)
	local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if humanoid then
		humanoid.Jumping:Connect(onJumping)
	end
	if infJumpActive then
		enableInfJump()
	end
end)

if LocalPlayer.Character then
	local humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if humanoid then
		humanoid.Jumping:Connect(onJumping)
	end
end

}

		minBtn.Text = "+"
	else
		TweenService:Create(main, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(guiW, guiH),
		}):Play()
		content.Visible = true
		minBtn.Text = "-"
	end
end)

-- Toggle helper
local function makeToggle(parent, y, labelText, isOn, onToggle)
	local row = Instance.new("Frame")
	row.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, y)
	row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, isMobile and 38 or 46)
	row.BackgroundColor3 = colors.dark
	row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
	row.ZIndex = 4
	row.Parent = parent
	Instance.new("UICorner", row).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)

	local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, -9)
	lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 0, 18)
	lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	lbl.Text = labelText
	lbl.TextColor3 = colors.white
	lbl.TextSize = isMobile and 12 or 14
	lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	lbl.ZIndex = 6
	lbl.Parent = row

	local bgToggle = Instance.new("Frame")
	bgToggle.Position = UDim2.new(1, -58, 0.5, -11)
	bgToggle.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 22)
	bgToggle.BackgroundColor3 = isOn and colors.accent or colors.toggleOff
	bgToggle.BorderSizePixel = 0
	bgToggle.ZIndex = 6
	bgToggle.Parent = row
	Instance.new("UICorner", bgToggle).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

	local dot = Instance.new("Frame")
	dot.Position = isOn and UDim2.new(1, -19, 0.5, -8) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -8)
	dot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16)
	dot.BackgroundColor3 = isOn and colors.white or colors.white
	dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
	dot.ZIndex = 7
	dot.Parent = bgToggle
	Instance.new("UICorner", dot).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

	local click = Instance.new("TextButton")
	click.Position = UDim2.new(1, -65, 0, 0)
	click.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(50, isMobile and 38 or 46)
	click.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	click.Text = ""
	click.ZIndex = 10
	click.Parent = row

	local function setVisual(state)
		local targetPos = state and UDim2.new(1, -19, 0.5, -8) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -8)
		local targetBg = state and colors.accent or colors.toggleOff
		TweenService:Create(dot, TweenInfo.new(0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
			Position = targetPos,
		}):Play()
		TweenService:Create(bgToggle, TweenInfo.new(0.16), { BackgroundColor3 = targetBg }):Play()
	end

	click.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		onToggle()
		local state = (labelText == "Anti Bat" and antiBatActive) or (labelText == "Inf Jump" and infJumpActive)
		setVisual(state)
		updateStatus()
		saveConfig()
	end)

	return setVisual
end

local setAntiVisual = makeToggle(content, isMobile and 40 or 46, "Anti Bat", config.AntiBatEnabled, function()
	if not antiBatActive then
		enableAntiBat()
		config.AntiBatEnabled = true
	else
		disableAntiBat()
		config.AntiBatEnabled = false
	end
end)

local setInfVisual = makeToggle(content, isMobile and 84 or 100, "Inf Jump", config.InfJumpEnabled, function()
	if not infJumpActive then
		enableInfJump()
		config.InfJumpEnabled = true
	else
		disableInfJump()
		config.InfJumpEnabled = false
	end
end)

-- Keybind row
local keyRow = Instance.new("Frame")
keyRow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, isMobile and 128 or 156)
keyRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, isMobile and 28 or 32)
keyRow.BackgroundColor3 = colors.dark
keyRow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
keyRow.ZIndex = 4
keyRow.Parent = content
Instance.new("UICorner", keyRow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)

local keyLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
keyLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0)
keyLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 1, 0)
keyLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
keyLbl.Text = "Keybind"
keyLbl.TextColor3 = colors.white
keyLbl.TextSize = isMobile and 11 or 13
keyLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
keyLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
keyLbl.ZIndex = 6
keyLbl.Parent = keyRow

local keyBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
keyBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
keyBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0.5, 0)
keyBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 20)
keyBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 25, 55)
keyBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
keyBtn.Text = config.Keybind
keyBtn.TextColor3 = colors.white
keyBtn.TextSize = isMobile and 10 or 12
keyBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
keyBtn.ZIndex = 6
keyBtn.Parent = keyRow
Instance.new("UICorner", keyBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

local listening = false
local listenConn = nil

keyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	if listening then
		if listenConn then
			listenConn:Disconnect()
			listenConn = nil
		end
		listening = false
		keyBtn.Text = config.Keybind
		keyBtn.TextColor3 = colors.white
		return
	end
	listening = true
	keyBtn.Text = "..."
	keyBtn.TextColor3 = colors.accent
	listenConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if not listening then return end
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
			if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
				listening = false
				keyBtn.Text = config.Keybind
				keyBtn.TextColor3 = colors.white
				if listenConn then
					listenConn:Disconnect()
					listenConn = nil
				end
				return
			end
			config.Keybind = input.KeyCode.Name
			keyBtn.Text = config.Keybind
			keyBtn.TextColor3 = colors.white
			listening = false
			if listenConn then
				listenConn:Disconnect()
				listenConn = nil
			end
			saveConfig()
		end
	end)
end)

_conns[#_conns + 1] = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
	if gp then return end
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and input.KeyCode.Name == config.Keybind then
		if not antiBatActive then
			enableAntiBat()
			config.AntiBatEnabled = true
		else
			disableAntiBat()
			config.AntiBatEnabled = false
		end
		setAntiVisual(antiBatActive)
		updateStatus()
		saveConfig()
	end
end)

	for _, entry in ipairs(bgBtnRefs) do
		local selected = (entry.id == (config.BackgroundId or ""))
		entry.stroke.Color = selected and colors.accent or Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 65)
		entry.stroke.Thickness = selected and 1.6 or 1
		entry.lbl.TextColor3 = selected and colors.accent or colors.gray
	end
end

		applyBackground(opt.id)
		refreshBgButtons()
		saveConfig()
	end)

	bgBtnRefs[#bgBtnRefs + 1] = { id = opt.id, stroke = stroke, lbl = lbl, btn = btn }
end
refreshBgButtons()

-- Drag
local dragging = false
local dragStart, startPos, touchIndex

local function clampPos(pos)
	local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
	local vs = cam and cam.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1920, 1080)
	local inset = GuiService:GetGuiInset()
	local x = math.clamp(pos.X.Offset + pos.X.Scale * vs.X, 0, math.max(0, vs.X - main.AbsoluteSize.X))
	local y = math.clamp(pos.Y.Offset + pos.Y.Scale * (vs.Y - inset.Y), 0, math.max(0, vs.Y - inset.Y - main.AbsoluteSize.Y))
	return UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
end

main.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
		dragging = true
		dragStart = input.Position
		startPos = main.Position
		touchIndex = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input.UserInputIndex or nil
	end
end)

_conns[#_conns + 1] = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
	if not dragging then return end
	local isTouch = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
	if isTouch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
		if isTouch and touchIndex and input.UserInputIndex ~= touchIndex then return end
		local delta = input.Position - dragStart
		main.Position = clampPos(UDim2.new(
			startPos.X.Scale,
			startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
			startPos.Y.Scale,
			startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
		))
	end
end)

_conns[#_conns + 1] = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
	if not dragging then return end
	local isTouch = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
	if isTouch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
		if isTouch and touchIndex and input.UserInputIndex ~= touchIndex then return end
		dragging = false
		touchIndex = nil
	end
end)

-- Init state
if config.AntiBatEnabled then
	enableAntiBat()
	setAntiVisual(true)
end
if config.InfJumpEnabled then
	enableInfJump()
	setInfVisual(true)
end
updateStatus()


local function setOpen(on)
	on = on == true
	if panelOpen == on then return end
	panelOpen = on
	main.Visible = on
end

return {
	Toggle = function() setOpen(not panelOpen); return panelOpen end,
	IsOpen = function() return panelOpen end,
	SetOpen = setOpen,
	Destroy = function()
		disableAntiBat()
		disableInfJump()
		for _, c in ipairs(_conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
		_conns = {}
		pcall(function() screenGui:Destroy() end)
	end,
}
end)()
_G._VioletteccAntiBatUIRuntime = _G._VioletteccAntiBatUI
end)


-- Settings launchers (top of Settings): press to open a tool window, press again to close.
do
    local function addLauncher(order, label, uiGlobal)
        local row = makeRowBase(pSettings, order)
        nameWithUnderline(row, label)

        local chip = Instance.new("Frame", row)
        chip.Size = UDim2.new(0, getMobileOptimized(58, 52), 0, getMobileOptimized(22, 20))
        chip.Position = UDim2.new(1, -(getMobileOptimized(58, 52) + 12), 0.5, -getMobileOptimized(11, 10))
        chip.BackgroundColor3 = ACC
        chip.BackgroundTransparency = 0.65
        chip.BorderSizePixel = 0
        chip.ZIndex = 6
        Instance.new("UICorner", chip).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        local chipLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", chip)
        chipLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        chipLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        chipLbl.Text = "OPEN"
        chipLbl.TextColor3 = WHITE
        chipLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        chipLbl.TextSize = getMobileOptimized(11, 10)
        chipLbl.ZIndex = 7

        local hit = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        hit.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        hit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        hit.Text = ""
        hit.AutoButtonColor = false
        hit.ZIndex = 8
        addPressPop(row, hit)

        local function paint(isOpen)
            chipLbl.Text = isOpen and "CLOSE" or "OPEN"
            chipLbl.TextColor3 = isOpen and Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) or WHITE
            TweenService:Create(chip, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundTransparency = isOpen and 0 or 0.65}):Play()
        end
        hit.Activated:Connect(function()
            local ui = _G[uiGlobal]
            if not ui then return end
            paint(ui.Toggle())
        end)
    end

    -- negative orders keep these above every other Settings row, in this order
    addLauncher(-4, "Anti Bat", "_VioletteccAntiBatUI")
end

local sLockUI = TR(pSettings, 5, "Lock UI", "", nil, _G._VynxUILocked, function(on)
    _G._VynxUILocked = on

    if _G._VynxRefreshHdrLockIcon then pcall(_G._VynxRefreshHdrLockIcon) end

end); VisualSetters.LockUI = sLockUI

_G._VioletteccIntroSong = _G._VioletteccIntroSong or "Song 1"

    _G._VioletteccIntroSong = "Song 1"
end

    "Off",
    "Song 1",

    INTRO_SONG_NAMES,

        _G._VioletteccIntroSong = nv
        _G._RaVeIntroSong  = nv
        if _G._VioletteccPlayIntroSong then pcall(_G._VioletteccPlayIntroSong, nv, true) end
        if _G.KRIXSaveNow then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNow) end
    end)

    if _introSongLbl then
        _introSongLbl.Text = tostring(_G._VioletteccIntroSong or "Song 1"):upper()
    end

local sSkipIntro = TR(pSettings, 7, "Skip Intro", "", nil, _G._VioletteccSkipIntro, function(on)
    _G._VioletteccSkipIntro = on and true or false
    if _G.KRIXSaveNowInstant then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNowInstant) end
    if _G.KRIXSaveNow then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNow) end
end); VisualSetters.SkipIntro = sSkipIntro

do
    local f = makeRowBase(pSettings, 8)
    nameWithUnderline(f, "UI Toggle Key")
    local bx = Instance.new("Frame", f)
    bx.Size = UDim2.new(0,getMobileOptimized(70,60),0,getMobileOptimized(26,22))
    bx.Position = UDim2.new(1,-getMobileOptimized(80,68),0.5,-getMobileOptimized(13,11))
    bx.BackgroundColor3 = BG_DARK; bx.BorderSizePixel = 0; bx.ZIndex = 6
    Instance.new("UICorner",bx).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10)
    local kt = Instance.new("TextLabel", bx)
    kt.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0); kt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    kt.Text = (Keybinds.UIToggle ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown) and _G._VioletteccKeyName(Keybinds.UIToggle) or "None"; kt.TextColor3 = ACC
    kt.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; kt.TextSize = getMobileOptimized(11,10); kt.ZIndex = 7
    kt.TextScaled = true
    do local c = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint", kt); c.MaxTextSize = getMobileOptimized(11,10); c.MinTextSize = 6 end
    bx.ClipsDescendants = true
    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", bx)
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0); btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    btn.Text = ""; btn.ZIndex = 8
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        kt.Text = "..."
        _G._VioletteccBeginKeyCapture()
        local conn; conn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(i, g)

            if i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard
               and true
               and not _isGamepadInput(i) then return end
            local kc = i.KeyCode
            if kc == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then kt.Text = (Keybinds.UIToggle ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown) and _G._VioletteccKeyName(Keybinds.UIToggle) or "None"; _G._VioletteccEndKeyCapture(); conn:Disconnect(); return end
            if kc == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace then
                Keybinds.UIToggle = Enum.KeyCode.Unknown; kt.Text = "None"
                _G._VioletteccEndKeyCapture(); conn:Disconnect()
                if _G.KRIXSaveNowInstant then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNowInstant) end
                if _G.KRIXSaveNow then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNow) end
                return
            end
            if kc == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then return end
            if _G._AdaptKeybindSteal then _G._AdaptKeybindSteal(kc, "UIToggle") end
            Keybinds.UIToggle = kc; kt.Text = _G._VioletteccKeyName(kc)
            _G._VioletteccEndKeyCapture()
            conn:Disconnect()
            if _G.KRIXSaveNowInstant then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNowInstant) end
            if _G.KRIXSaveNow        then pcall(_G.KRIXSaveNow) end
        end)
    end)
    _G._VynxRefreshUIToggleKey = function()
        kt.Text = (Keybinds.UIToggle ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown) and _G._VioletteccKeyName(Keybinds.UIToggle) or "None"
    end
end
