    Panel.Position = winPos
    Panel.Visible = false

    return ScreenGui, Panel, KeyBtn, keyStroke, UnwalkBtn, PANEL_W, PANEL_H
end

local ScreenGui, Panel, KeyBtn, keyStroke, UnwalkBtn, PANEL_W, PANEL_H = buildUI()

UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
    if hubStale() then return end
    if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard
       and true
       and not _isGamepadInput(input) then return end

    if waitingForKey then
        if gpe then return end
        toggleKey = input.KeyCode
        if KeyBtn then KeyBtn.Text = "[ " .. toggleKey.Name .. " ]" end
        if keyStroke then tween(keyStroke, 0.2, {Color = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60), Thickness = 1}) end
        waitingForKey = false
        saveSettings()
        return
    end

    if gpe then return end
    if _G._VioletteccKeyCaptureActive and _G._VioletteccKeyCaptureActive() then return end
    if input.KeyCode == toggleKey then
        setDesync(not desyncActive)
        if updateVisual then updateVisual() end
    end
end)

_G.KrixDesyncToggle = function(on)
    setDesync(on and true or false)
    if updateVisual then updateVisual() end
end


_G.KrixDesyncShowPanel = function()
    Panel.Visible = true
    Panel.Size = UDim2.new(0, PANEL_W, 0, 0)
    Panel.Position = UDim2.new(winPos.X.Scale, winPos.X.Offset, winPos.Y.Scale, winPos.Y.Offset + (PANEL_H / 2))
    TS:Create(Panel, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, PANEL_W, 0, PANEL_H),
        Position = winPos
    }):Play()
end

_G.KrixDesyncHidePanel = function()
    TS:Create(Panel, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, PANEL_W, 0, 0)
    }):Play()
    task.delay(0.27, function() Panel.Visible = false end)
end

_G.KrixDesyncTogglePanel = function()
    if Panel.Visible then
        _G.KrixDesyncHidePanel()
    else
        _G.KrixDesyncShowPanel()
    end
end

end)()

local function playVioletteLoadIntro(onFinished)
    if _G._VioletteccLoadIntroCleanup then
        pcall(_G._VioletteccLoadIntroCleanup)
    end

    -- Start the supplied intro track. It is embedded in the script itself.
    if _G._VioletteccPlayIntroSong then
        pcall(_G._VioletteccPlayIntroSong, _G._VioletteccIntroSong or "Song 1", false)
    end

    local playerGui = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    local oldIntro = playerGui:FindFirstChild("LightHubIntro") or playerGui:FindFirstChild("VioletteLoadIntro")
    if oldIntro then oldIntro:Destroy() end
    local oldBlur = Lighting:FindFirstChild("LightHubIntroBlur") or Lighting:FindFirstChild("VioletteLoadIntroBlur")
    if oldBlur then oldBlur:Destroy() end

    local introGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    introGui.Name = "LightHubIntro"
    introGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    introGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    introGui.DisplayOrder = 1000005
    introGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    if _G._VioletteccMountOnTop then
        _G._VioletteccMountOnTop(introGui, 1000005)
    else
        introGui.Parent = playerGui
    end

    local isMobile = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled
    local PEARL = Color3.fromRGB(255, 253, 246)
    local CHAMPAGNE = Color3.fromRGB(244, 211, 151)
    local SKY = Color3.fromRGB(157, 216, 246)
    local ICE = Color3.fromRGB(217, 241, 255)
    local NAVY = Color3.fromRGB(3, 9, 23)

    local backdrop = Instance.new("Frame")
    backdrop.Name = "AngelBackdrop"
    backdrop.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    backdrop.BackgroundColor3 = NAVY
    backdrop.BackgroundTransparency = 0.02
    backdrop.BorderSizePixel = 0
    backdrop.ZIndex = 1000
    backdrop.Parent = introGui
    local bgGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", backdrop)
    bgGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(2, 5, 17)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.46, Color3.fromRGB(12, 34, 59)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(3, 8, 23)),
    })
    bgGradient.Rotation = 90

    -- Wide celestial bloom behind the angel emblem.
    local bloom = Instance.new("Frame", backdrop)
    bloom.Name = "CelestialBloom"
    bloom.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    bloom.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.42)
    bloom.Size = isMobile and UDim2.fromOffset(360, 300) or UDim2.fromOffset(700, 520)
    bloom.BackgroundColor3 = CHAMPAGNE
    bloom.BackgroundTransparency = 0.88
    bloom.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bloom.ZIndex = 1001
    Instance.new("UICorner", bloom).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local bloomGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", bloom)
    bloomGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, CHAMPAGNE),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, SKY),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, PEARL),
    })
    bloomGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.25),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, 0.78),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
    })

    -- Rotating rays create the strong light-burst reveal.
    local rays = {}
    for i = 1, 18 do
        local ray = Instance.new("Frame", backdrop)
        ray.Name = "DivineRay" .. i
        ray.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        ray.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.415)
        ray.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(i % 3 == 0 and 4 or 2, isMobile and 300 or 500)
        ray.BackgroundColor3 = (i % 2 == 0) and PEARL or SKY
        ray.BackgroundTransparency = 0.91
        ray.BorderSizePixel = 0
        ray.Rotation = (i - 1) * 10
        ray.ZIndex = 1001
        local rg = Instance.new("UIGradient", ray)
        rg.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.12),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
        })
        rays[#rays + 1] = ray
    end

    local function makeRing(name, diameter, color, thickness, transparency)
        local ring = Instance.new("Frame", backdrop)
        ring.Name = name
        ring.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        ring.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.415)
        ring.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(diameter, diameter)
        ring.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ring.BorderSizePixel = 0
        ring.ZIndex = 1002
        Instance.new("UICorner", ring).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", ring)
        stroke.Color = color
        stroke.Thickness = thickness
        stroke.Transparency = transparency
        stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        return ring, stroke
    end

    local outerRing, outerRingStroke = makeRing("CelestialOuterRing", isMobile and 300 or 440, SKY, 1.5, 0.52)
    local middleRing, middleRingStroke = makeRing("CelestialMiddleRing", isMobile and 250 or 360, CHAMPAGNE, 2, 0.34)
    local halo, haloStroke = makeRing("AngelHalo", isMobile and 192 or 274, PEARL, 2.5, 0.12)
    local innerHalo, innerHaloStroke = makeRing("AngelHaloInner", isMobile and 166 or 244, SKY, 1.2, 0.42)

    -- Symmetrical feathered wings, built from glowing GUI feathers (no external image needed).
    local wingFeathers = {}
    for _, side in ipairs({-1, 1}) do
        for i = 1, 10 do
            local xOffset = (isMobile and 34 or 62) + (i - 1) * (isMobile and 7 or 12)
            local yOffset = (isMobile and 5 or 9) + (i - 1) * (isMobile and 3.5 or 5.2)
            local length = (isMobile and 57 or 102) - (i - 1) * (isMobile and 2.5 or 3.8)
            local feather = Instance.new("Frame", backdrop)
            feather.Name = (side < 0 and "Left" or "Right") .. "AngelFeather" .. i
            feather.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            feather.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, side * xOffset, 0.415, yOffset)
            feather.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(length, isMobile and 8 or 13)
            feather.Rotation = side * (12 + i * 4)
            feather.BackgroundColor3 = PEARL
            feather.BackgroundTransparency = 0.20
            feather.BorderSizePixel = 0
            feather.ZIndex = 1003
            Instance.new("UICorner", feather).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
            local fg = Instance.new("UIGradient", feather)
            fg.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, SKY),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.52, PEARL),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, CHAMPAGNE),
            })
            local fs = Instance.new("UIStroke", feather)
            fs.Color = ICE
            fs.Thickness = 0.65
            fs.Transparency = 0.42
            local pop = Instance.new("UIScale", feather)
            pop.Scale = 0.35
            TweenService:Create(pop, TweenInfo.new(0.48 + i * 0.018, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Scale = 1}):Play()
            wingFeathers[#wingFeathers + 1] = {
                frame = feather,
                stroke = fs,
                baseRotation = feather.Rotation,
                phase = i * 0.63 + side,
            }
        end
    end

    -- A small floating star crest above the title.
    local crest = Instance.new("TextLabel", backdrop)
    crest.Name = "DivineCrest"
    crest.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    crest.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.245)
    crest.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(isMobile and 46 or 64, isMobile and 46 or 64)
    crest.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    crest.Text = "✦"
    crest.TextColor3 = CHAMPAGNE
    crest.TextStrokeColor3 = PEARL
    crest.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.48
    crest.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    crest.TextSize = isMobile and 32 or 46
    crest.TextTransparency = 1
    crest.ZIndex = 1008

    local kicker = Instance.new("TextLabel", backdrop)
    kicker.Name = "DivineKicker"
    kicker.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    kicker.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.305)
    kicker.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0, 22)
    kicker.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    kicker.Text = "✧  THE CELESTIAL EXPERIENCE  ✧"
    kicker.TextColor3 = SKY
    kicker.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    kicker.TextSize = isMobile and 9 or 12
    kicker.TextTransparency = 1
    kicker.ZIndex = 1008

    local titleWrap = Instance.new("Frame", backdrop)
    titleWrap.Name = "LightHubTitleWrap"
    titleWrap.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    titleWrap.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.415)
    titleWrap.Size = isMobile and UDim2.fromOffset(350, 94) or UDim2.fromOffset(760, 142)
    titleWrap.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    titleWrap.ZIndex = 1010
    local titleScale = Instance.new("UIScale", titleWrap)
    titleScale.Scale = 0.48

    local titleGlow = Instance.new("TextLabel", titleWrap)
    titleGlow.Name = "LightHubTitleGlow"
    titleGlow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    titleGlow.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.48)
    titleGlow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 12, 0.84, 12)
    titleGlow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    titleGlow.Text = "LIGHT HUB"
    titleGlow.TextColor3 = CHAMPAGNE
    titleGlow.TextTransparency = 0.70
    titleGlow.TextStrokeColor3 = SKY
    titleGlow.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.72
    titleGlow.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    titleGlow.TextScaled = true
    titleGlow.TextWrapped = false
    titleGlow.ZIndex = 1010

    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", titleWrap)
    title.Name = "LightHubTitle"
    title.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    title.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.46)
    title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.80, 0)
    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title.Text = "LIGHT HUB"
    title.TextColor3 = PEARL
    title.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(52, 86, 112)
    title.TextTransparency = 1
    title.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
    title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    title.TextScaled = true
    title.TextWrapped = false
    title.ZIndex = 1011
    local titleGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", title)
    titleGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, ICE),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.32, PEARL),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, CHAMPAGNE),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.76, SKY),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, PEARL),
    })
    titleGradient.Rotation = 0

    local subtitle = Instance.new("TextLabel", backdrop)
    subtitle.Name = "LightHubSubtitle"
    subtitle.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    subtitle.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.415, isMobile and 58 or 91)
    subtitle.Size = UDim2.new(0.92, 0, 0, 24)
    subtitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    subtitle.Text = "ANGEL POWER  •  ASCEND BEYOND LIMITS"
    subtitle.TextColor3 = SKY
    subtitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    subtitle.TextSize = isMobile and 10 or 14
    subtitle.TextTransparency = 1
    subtitle.ZIndex = 1011

    local lowerLine = Instance.new("TextLabel", backdrop)
    lowerLine.Name = "LightHubLowerLine"
    lowerLine.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    lowerLine.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.415, isMobile and 83 or 119)
    lowerLine.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0, 18)
    lowerLine.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lowerLine.Text = "PEARL  •  CHAMPAGNE  •  SKY"
    lowerLine.TextColor3 = CHAMPAGNE
    lowerLine.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    lowerLine.TextSize = isMobile and 8 or 10
    lowerLine.TextTransparency = 1
    lowerLine.ZIndex = 1011

    -- Floating sparks in front of the wings and behind the wordmark.
    local particleField = Instance.new("Frame", backdrop)
    particleField.Name = "AngelParticles"
    particleField.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    particleField.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    particleField.ClipsDescendants = true
    particleField.ZIndex = 1006
    local particles = {}
    for i = 1, (isMobile and 30 or 54) do
        local p = Instance.new("Frame", particleField)
        p.Name = "AngelSpark" .. i
        p.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        p.Position = UDim2.fromScale((i * 41 % 100) / 100, (i * 67 % 100) / 100)
        p.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(i % 7 == 0 and 4 or 2, i % 7 == 0 and 4 or 2)
        p.BackgroundColor3 = i % 3 == 0 and SKY or PEARL
        p.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
        p.BorderSizePixel = 0
        p.ZIndex = 1006
        Instance.new("UICorner", p).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        particles[i] = {
            frame = p,
            x = (i * 41 % 100) / 100,
            y = (i * 67 % 100) / 100,
            speed = 0.012 + (i % 5) * 0.006,
            phase = i * 0.87,
            opacity = 0.42 + (i % 5) * 0.08,
        }
    end

    local topBar = Instance.new("Frame", backdrop)
    topBar.Name = "CelestialTopLine"
    topBar.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    topBar.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0)
    topBar.Size = UDim2.new(0.86, 0, 0, 2)
    topBar.BackgroundColor3 = CHAMPAGNE
    topBar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
    topBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    topBar.ZIndex = 1012
    local topGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", topBar)
    topGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
    })

    local tapPrompt = Instance.new("TextLabel", backdrop)
    tapPrompt.Name = "SkipPrompt"
    tapPrompt.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
    tapPrompt.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -38)
    tapPrompt.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0, 22)
    tapPrompt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    tapPrompt.Text = isMobile and "TAP ANYWHERE TO ENTER" or "CLICK ANYWHERE  •  PRESS ANY KEY"
    tapPrompt.TextColor3 = ICE
    tapPrompt.TextTransparency = 1
    tapPrompt.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    tapPrompt.TextSize = isMobile and 10 or 12
    tapPrompt.ZIndex = 1012

    local blur = Instance.new("BlurEffect")
    blur.Name = "LightHubIntroBlur"
    blur.Size = 0
    blur.Parent = Lighting

    local exitFade = Instance.new("Frame", backdrop)
    exitFade.Name = "IntroExitFade"
    exitFade.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    exitFade.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(1, 4, 13)
    exitFade.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    exitFade.BorderSizePixel = 0
    exitFade.ZIndex = 1100

    local finished = false
    local floatConn
    local keyConn

    local function finish()
        if finished then return end
        finished = true
        if floatConn then floatConn:Disconnect(); floatConn = nil end
        if keyConn then keyConn:Disconnect(); keyConn = nil end

        -- Stop the supplied music immediately on click/tap/key; no audio keeps playing under the menu.
        if _G._VioletteccStopIntroSong then
            pcall(_G._VioletteccStopIntroSong)
        elseif _G._VioletteccFadeIntroSong then
            pcall(_G._VioletteccFadeIntroSong, 0.12)
        end

        TweenService:Create(exitFade, TweenInfo.new(0.26, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            BackgroundTransparency = 0,
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(titleScale, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
            Scale = 0.92,
        }):Play()
        task.delay(0.29, function()
            if introGui and introGui.Parent then introGui:Destroy() end
            if blur and blur.Parent then blur:Destroy() end
            _G._VioletteccLoadIntroCleanup = nil
            if gui and gui.Parent then gui.Enabled = true end
            if _G._VioletteccForceHudReveal then pcall(_G._VioletteccForceHudReveal, true) end
            if onFinished then pcall(onFinished) end
        end)
    end

    _G._VioletteccLoadIntroCleanup = function()
        if finished then return end
        finished = true
        if floatConn then floatConn:Disconnect(); floatConn = nil end
        if keyConn then keyConn:Disconnect(); keyConn = nil end
        if _G._VioletteccStopIntroSong then pcall(_G._VioletteccStopIntroSong) end
        if introGui and introGui.Parent then introGui:Destroy() end
        if blur and blur.Parent then blur:Destroy() end
        _G._VioletteccLoadIntroCleanup = nil
    end

    -- Cinematic entrance: blur clears, wings open, rings flare, then the title lands.
    TweenService:Create(blur, TweenInfo.new(0.72, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = 15}):Play()
    task.delay(0.06, function()
        if finished then return end
        TweenService:Create(blur, TweenInfo.new(0.78, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = 0}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(titleScale, TweenInfo.new(0.72, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Scale = 1.07}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(title, TweenInfo.new(0.55, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            TextTransparency = 0,
            TextStrokeTransparency = 0.18,
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(titleGlow, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {TextTransparency = 0.58, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.55}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(crest, TweenInfo.new(0.55, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {TextTransparency = 0.05}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(kicker, TweenInfo.new(0.55), {TextTransparency = 0.16}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(subtitle, TweenInfo.new(0.62), {TextTransparency = 0.08}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(lowerLine, TweenInfo.new(0.72), {TextTransparency = 0.25}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(tapPrompt, TweenInfo.new(0.8), {TextTransparency = 0.22}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(haloStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.72), {Transparency = 0.08}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(innerHaloStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.82), {Transparency = 0.3}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(middleRingStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.85), {Transparency = 0.24}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(outerRingStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.92), {Transparency = 0.42}):Play()
        TweenService:Create(bloom, TweenInfo.new(0.85), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.80}):Play()
        task.delay(0.72, function()
            if not finished and titleScale and titleScale.Parent then
                TweenService:Create(titleScale, TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Scale = 1}):Play()
            end
        end)
    end)

    -- A single expanding halo gives the reveal a powerful celestial impact.
    local shockwave = Instance.new("Frame", backdrop)
    shockwave.Name = "AngelShockwave"
    shockwave.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    shockwave.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.415)
    shockwave.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(36, 36)
    shockwave.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    shockwave.BorderSizePixel = 0
    shockwave.ZIndex = 1002
    Instance.new("UICorner", shockwave).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local shockStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", shockwave)
    shockStroke.Color = PEARL
    shockStroke.Thickness = 2.5
    shockStroke.Transparency = 0.15
    TweenService:Create(shockwave, TweenInfo.new(1.0, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(isMobile and 360 or 620, isMobile and 360 or 620),
    }):Play()
    TweenService:Create(shockStroke, TweenInfo.new(1.0, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Transparency = 1,
        Thickness = 0.5,
    }):Play()

    local started = os.clock()
    floatConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if finished then return end
        local elapsed = os.clock() - started
        local pulse = (math.sin(elapsed * 2.5) + 1) * 0.5
        halo.Rotation = elapsed * 7
        innerHalo.Rotation = -elapsed * 10
        middleRing.Rotation = elapsed * 4
        outerRing.Rotation = -elapsed * 2.4
        bloom.BackgroundTransparency = 0.82 + pulse * 0.07
        crest.Rotation = math.sin(elapsed * 0.9) * 9
        titleGlow.TextTransparency = 0.54 + pulse * 0.12
        for i, ray in ipairs(rays) do
            ray.Rotation = (i - 1) * 10 + elapsed * (i % 2 == 0 and 2.4 or -2.0)
            ray.BackgroundTransparency = 0.91 + pulse * 0.035
        end
        for _, feather in ipairs(wingFeathers) do
            feather.frame.Rotation = feather.baseRotation + math.sin(elapsed * 1.5 + feather.phase) * 1.1
            feather.frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.17 + pulse * 0.075
            feather.stroke.Transparency = 0.34 + pulse * 0.13
        end
        for _, item in ipairs(particles) do
            local x = (item.x + elapsed * item.speed * 0.15) % 1.05 - 0.025
            local y = (item.y + math.sin(elapsed * 0.7 + item.phase) * 0.04) % 1.05 - 0.025
            local twinkle = (math.sin(elapsed * 2.1 + item.phase) + 1) * 0.5
            item.frame.Position = UDim2.fromScale(x, y)
            item.frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - (0.52 + twinkle * 0.28) * item.opacity
        end
        tapPrompt.TextTransparency = 0.2 + pulse * 0.34
    end)

    -- The full-screen transparent button makes tapping/clicking anywhere skip the intro.
    local skipBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", backdrop)
    skipBtn.Name = "SkipCapture"
    skipBtn.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    skipBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    skipBtn.Text = ""
    skipBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    skipBtn.ZIndex = 1050
    skipBtn.Activated:Connect(finish)

    keyConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if finished then return end
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            or (input.UserInputType.Name and input.UserInputType.Name:sub(1, 7) == "Gamepad") then
            finish()
        end
    end)

    task.delay(7, finish)
end


-- Embedded LIGHT HUB intro audio is initialized before the intro can start.

        ["Song 1"] = { file = "LIGHT_HUB_ANGEL_INTRO.mp3" },
    }

qqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqq
qqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqqo=
]=]

    local function decodeBase64(data)
        local alphabet = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
        local lookup = {}
        for i = 1, #alphabet do
            lookup[alphabet:sub(i, i)] = i - 1
        end
        local out = {}
        local buffer, bits = 0, 0
        for i = 1, #data do
            local ch = data:sub(i, i)
            if ch == "=" then break end
            local value = lookup[ch]
            if value ~= nil then
                buffer = buffer * 64 + value
                bits = bits + 6
                if bits >= 8 then
                    bits = bits - 8
                    local byte = math.floor(buffer / (2 ^ bits)) % 256
                    out[#out + 1] = string.char(byte)
                    buffer = buffer % (2 ^ bits)
                end
            end
        end
        return table.concat(out)
    end

    local INTRO_VOLUME = 0.72
    local INTRO_FILE = "LIGHT_HUB_ANGEL_INTRO.mp3"
    _G._VioletteccIntroSong = _G._VioletteccIntroSong or "Song 1"
    _G._VioletteccIntroSongCache = _G._VioletteccIntroSongCache or {}
    _G._VioletteccIntroSongToken = _G._VioletteccIntroSongToken or 0

    if _G._VioletteccIntroSong ~= "Off" and _G._VioletteccIntroSong ~= "Song 1" then
        _G._VioletteccIntroSong = "Song 1"
    end

    _G._VioletteccStopIntroSong = function()
        _G._VioletteccIntroSongToken = (_G._VioletteccIntroSongToken or 0) + 1
        local snd = _G._VioletteccIntroSound
        _G._VioletteccIntroSound = nil
        if snd then
            pcall(function() snd:Stop() end)
            pcall(function() snd:Destroy() end)
        end
    end

    _G._VioletteccFadeIntroSong = function(dur)
        dur = tonumber(dur) or 0.2
        local snd = _G._VioletteccIntroSound
        _G._VioletteccIntroSongToken = (_G._VioletteccIntroSongToken or 0) + 1
        _G._VioletteccIntroSound = nil
        if not snd then return end
        pcall(function()
            TweenService:Create(snd, TweenInfo.new(dur, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Volume = 0}):Play()
        end)
        task.delay(dur + 0.04, function()
            pcall(function() snd:Stop() end)
            pcall(function() snd:Destroy() end)
        end)
    end

    _G._VioletteccGetIntroSongAsset = function(name)
        local entry = _G._VioletteccIntroSongs[name]
        if not entry then return nil end
        if _G._VioletteccIntroSongCache[name] then return _G._VioletteccIntroSongCache[name] end

        local asset = getcustomasset or getsynasset
        if type(asset) ~= "function" or type(writefile) ~= "function" then return nil end

        local onDisk = false
        pcall(function()
            onDisk = type(isfile) == "function" and isfile(entry.file) and true or false
        end)
        if not onDisk then
            local bytes = decodeBase64(INTRO_AUDIO_B64)
            if type(bytes) ~= "string" or #bytes < 1000 then return nil end
            local okWrite = pcall(writefile, entry.file, bytes)
            if not okWrite then return nil end
        end

        local ok, id = pcall(asset, entry.file)
        if not ok or not id then return nil end
        _G._VioletteccIntroSongCache[name] = id
        return id
    end

    _G._VioletteccPlayIntroSong = function(name, preview)
        _G._VioletteccStopIntroSong()
        if name == nil then name = _G._VioletteccIntroSong end
        if name == "Off" then _G._VioletteccIntroSong = "Off"; return end
        if not _G._VioletteccIntroSongs[name] then return end
        _G._VioletteccIntroSong = name

        local token = _G._VioletteccIntroSongToken
        task.spawn(function()
            local id = _G._VioletteccGetIntroSongAsset(name)
            if token ~= _G._VioletteccIntroSongToken or not id then return end
            local snd = Instance.new("Sound")
            snd.Name = preview and "LightHubIntroPreview" or "LightHubIntroMusic"
            snd.SoundId = id
            snd.Volume = INTRO_VOLUME
            snd.Looped = false
            snd.Parent = SoundService
            _G._VioletteccIntroSound = snd
            pcall(function() snd:Play() end)
            snd.Ended:Connect(function()
                if _G._VioletteccIntroSound == snd then
                    _G._VioletteccIntroSound = nil
                    pcall(function() snd:Destroy() end)
                end
            end)
        end)
    end

    _G._RaVeIntroSong = _G._VioletteccIntroSong
    _G._RaVeIntroSongOrder = _G._VioletteccIntroSongOrder
    _G._RaVeIntroSongs = _G._VioletteccIntroSongs
    _G._RaVePlayIntroSong = _G._VioletteccPlayIntroSong
    _G._RaVeStopIntroSong = _G._VioletteccStopIntroSong
    _G._RaVeGetIntroSongAsset = _G._VioletteccGetIntroSongAsset
end)()


task.defer(function()
    local hubGui = gui
    local hubRevealed = false
    local function revealHub()
        if hubRevealed then return end
        hubRevealed = true
        _G._VioletteccIntroActive = false
        -- Never inherit a stale "hidden while intro was running" save.
        -- Only an explicit user hide keeps the hub closed after intro.
        _G._VioletteccWantMinimized = (_G._VioletteccUserHidHub == true)
        if hubGui then hubGui.Enabled = true end
        if _G._VioletteccForceHudReveal then
            pcall(_G._VioletteccForceHudReveal)
        else
            local stealBar = hubGui and hubGui:FindFirstChild("StealBar")
            if stealBar then stealBar.Visible = true end
            if _G._VynxApplyHideMobile then
                pcall(_G._VynxApplyHideMobile, _G._VezyHideSideBtns == true)
            elseif _G._VezyMobileButtonsFrame then
                _G._VezyMobileButtonsFrame.Visible = _G._VezyHideSideBtns ~= true
            end
            if _G._VioletteccUserHidHub ~= true then
                local outerRef = _G.VynxOuterRef
                if outerRef and outerRef.Parent then
                    if _G._VioletteccSetHubVisible then pcall(_G._VioletteccSetHubVisible, true, false)
                    else outerRef.Visible = true end
                end
            end
        end
        if _G._VioletteccRevealPanels then pcall(_G._VioletteccRevealPanels) end
        -- Extra safety nets for executors (e.g. Delta) that delay ScreenGui parenting
        task.delay(0.35, function()
            if _G._VioletteccForceHudReveal then pcall(_G._VioletteccForceHudReveal, true) end
        end)
        task.delay(1.0, function()
            if _G._VioletteccForceHudReveal then pcall(_G._VioletteccForceHudReveal, true) end
        end)
    end

    if _G._VioletteccSkipIntro == true then
        revealHub()
        return
    end

    _G._VioletteccIntroActive = true
    if hubGui then hubGui.Enabled = false end
    for _, key in ipairs({ "duelLagger", "speedBypass" }) do
        if _G._VioletteccShowPanel then pcall(_G._VioletteccShowPanel, key, false) end
    end
    playVioletteLoadIntro(revealHub)
    -- Same completion safety net used by the reference script's intro waiters:
    -- a failed optional audio/tween callback can never leave the HUD hidden.
    task.delay(8, revealHub)
    task.delay(8.1, function()
        if _G._VioletteccForceHudReveal then pcall(_G._VioletteccForceHudReveal, true) end
    end)
end)




;(function()
    local Players     = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService  = game:GetService("RunService")
    local LP          = Players.LocalPlayer

    local VIOLETTE_COLOR   = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    local DISCORD_TEXT  = "LIGHT HUB"

    local selfSpeedLabel = nil
    local otherLabels = {}

    local function buildSelfTag(char)
        if not char then return end
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:WaitForChild("Head", 5)
        if not head then return end
        local old = head:FindFirstChild("VioletteHeadBB")
        if old then old:Destroy() end

        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui", head)
        bb.Name = "VioletteHeadBB"
        bb.Size = UDim2.fromScale(3.2, 1.48)
        bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.45, 0)
        bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
        bb.ResetOnSpawn = false
        bb.LightInfluence = 0
        bb.MaxDistance = 150

        local card = Instance.new("Frame", bb)
        card.Name = "VioletteCard"
        card.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(12, 6, 25)
        card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
        card.BorderSizePixel = 0
        Instance.new("UICorner", card).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
        local cardStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", card)
        cardStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(149, 90, 255)
        cardStroke.Thickness = 2
        cardStroke.Transparency = 0.08

        local val = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
        val.Name = "Speed"
        val.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0.46, 0)
        val.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 2)
        val.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        val.Text = "0.0"
        val.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        val.TextScaled = true
        val.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(193, 143, 255)
        val.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.55
        val.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 8, 60)
        local vcon = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint", val)
        vcon.MinTextSize = 6
        vcon.MaxTextSize = 22

        local divider = Instance.new("Frame", card)
        divider.Name = "Divider"
        divider.Size = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 1)
        divider.Position = UDim2.new(0, 9, 0.53, 0)
        divider.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(148, 86, 255)
        divider.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
        divider.BorderSizePixel = 0

        local slogan = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
        slogan.Name = "Slogan"
        slogan.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0.34, 0)
        slogan.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.59, 0)
        slogan.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        slogan.Text = "LIGHT HUB  ·  ANGEL EDITION"
        slogan.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        slogan.TextScaled = true
        slogan.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(178, 122, 255)
        slogan.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.72
        slogan.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 5, 50)
        local scon = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint", slogan)
        scon.MinTextSize = 5
        scon.MaxTextSize = 15

        selfSpeedLabel = val
        -- Respawned tags pick up the current theme immediately
        if _G._VioletteccSetTagColor and _G._VioletteccAccent then
            pcall(_G._VioletteccSetTagColor, _G._VioletteccAccent)
        end
    end

    local function buildOtherTag(player)
        local char = player.Character
        if not char then return end
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not head then return end
        local old = head:FindFirstChild("VioletteSpeedBB")
        if old then old:Destroy() end

        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            pcall(function()
                hum.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
                hum.NameDisplayDistance = 0
                hum.HealthDisplayDistance = 0
            end)
        end

        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui", head)
        bb.Name = "VioletteSpeedBB"
        bb.Size = UDim2.fromScale(2.5, 1.02)
        bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.6, 0)
        bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
        bb.ResetOnSpawn = false
        bb.LightInfluence = 0
        bb.MaxDistance = 220

        local val = Instance.new("TextLabel", bb)
        val.Name = "Speed"
        val.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        val.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        val.Text = "0"
        val.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        val.TextScaled = true
        val.TextColor3 = VIOLETTE_COLOR
        val.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
        val.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        local con = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint", val)
        con.MinTextSize = 7
        con.MaxTextSize = 21

        otherLabels[player] = val
    end

    local function watchPlayer(player)
        if player == LP then return end
        if player.Character then task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.3); pcall(buildOtherTag, player) end) end
        player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            task.wait(0.4)
            pcall(buildOtherTag, player)
        end)
    end

    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do watchPlayer(player) end
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(watchPlayer)
    Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player) otherLabels[player] = nil end)

    if LP.Character then
        task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.3); pcall(buildSelfTag, LP.Character) end)
    end
    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        task.wait(0.3)
        pcall(buildSelfTag, char)
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(1) do
            local char = LP.Character
            local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if head and not head:FindFirstChild("VioletteHeadBB") then
                pcall(buildSelfTag, char)
            end
            for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if player ~= LP then
                    local pc = player.Character
                    local ph = pc and pc:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    if ph and not ph:FindFirstChild("VioletteSpeedBB") then
                        pcall(buildOtherTag, player)
                    elseif pc then
                        local phum = pc:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if phum and phum.DisplayDistanceType ~= Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None then
                            pcall(function()
                                phum.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
                                phum.NameDisplayDistance = 0
                                phum.HealthDisplayDistance = 0
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if selfSpeedLabel and selfSpeedLabel.Parent then
            local char = LP.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hrp then
                local routing = (_G._VioletteccAutoPathActive and _G._VioletteccAutoPathActive()) or false
                local aimbotting = (_G._VioletteccGetAimbotSpeed and _G._VioletteccGetAimbotSpeed()) ~= nil
                if humanoid and not routing and not aimbotting
                    and humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude == 0 then
                    if selfSpeedLabel.Text ~= "0.0" then selfSpeedLabel.Text = "0.0" end
                else
                    local vel = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                    local horizontal = Vector3.new(vel.X, 0, vel.Z).Magnitude

                    local ceiling = 0
                    if not routing then
                        ceiling = tonumber(_G._VioletteccGetSpeed and _G._VioletteccGetSpeed()) or 0
                        if ceiling > 0 then
                            horizontal = (horizontal > 0.15) and ceiling or 0
                        end
                    end

                    if horizontal < 0.15 then horizontal = 0 end
                    local speedTxt = string.format("%.1f", horizontal)
                    if selfSpeedLabel.Text ~= speedTxt then selfSpeedLabel.Text = speedTxt end
                end
            end
        end

        local SPEED_TAG_NEAR = 24
        local SPEED_TAG_FAR  = 160
        local SPEED_TAG_MAX  = 1.9
        local speedTagCam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local camCF = speedTagCam and speedTagCam.CFrame

        for player, label in pairs(otherLabels) do
            if label and label.Parent then
                local pc = player.Character
                local prp = pc and pc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if prp then
                    local vel = prp.AssemblyLinearVelocity or prp.Velocity
                    local tagTxt = string.format("%d", math.floor(Vector3.new(vel.X, 0, vel.Z).Magnitude + 0.5))
                    if label.Text ~= tagTxt then label.Text = tagTxt end

                    local bb = label.Parent
                    if camCF and bb and bb:IsA("BillboardGui") then
                        local dist = (camCF.Position - prp.Position).Magnitude
                        local grow = math.clamp(dist / SPEED_TAG_NEAR, 1, SPEED_TAG_FAR / SPEED_TAG_NEAR)
                        grow = math.min(grow, SPEED_TAG_MAX)
                        local tagSize = UDim2.fromScale(2.5 * grow, 1.02 * grow)
                        if bb.Size ~= tagSize then bb.Size = tagSize end
                    end
                end
            else
                otherLabels[player] = nil
            end
        end
    end))

    _G._VioletteccHeadTagColor = VIOLETTE_COLOR
    _G._VioletteccDiscordText = DISCORD_TEXT

    _G._VioletteccSetTagColor = function(c)
        if typeof(c) ~= "Color3" then return end
        VIOLETTE_COLOR = c
        _G._VioletteccHeadTagColor = c
        if selfSpeedLabel and selfSpeedLabel.Parent then
            selfSpeedLabel.TextColor3 = c
            local card = selfSpeedLabel.Parent
            local disc = card:FindFirstChild("Discord")
            if disc then disc.TextColor3 = c end
            -- Billboard card: panel, outline, divider, slogan follow the theme
            local T = _G._VioletteccThemeColor
            if T then
                card.BackgroundColor3 = T(Color3.fromRGB(12, 6, 25))
                local st = card:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                if st then st.Color = T(Color3.fromRGB(149, 90, 255)) end
                local dv = card:FindFirstChild("Divider")
                if dv then dv.BackgroundColor3 = T(Color3.fromRGB(148, 86, 255)) end
                local sl = card:FindFirstChild("Slogan")
                if sl then
                    sl.TextColor3 = T(Color3.fromRGB(178, 122, 255))
                    sl.TextStrokeColor3 = T(Color3.fromRGB(25, 5, 50))
                end
                selfSpeedLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = T(Color3.fromRGB(30, 8, 60))
            end
        end
        for _, label in pairs(otherLabels) do
            if label and label.Parent then label.TextColor3 = c end
        end
    end
    if _G._VioletteccAccent then pcall(_G._VioletteccSetTagColor, _G._VioletteccAccent) end
end)()

;(function()

    _G._VioletteccFakeSpeed = tonumber(_G._VioletteccFakeSpeed) or 16

    local HIJACK = _G._VelHijackStore
    if type(HIJACK) ~= "table" then
        HIJACK = { v = Vector3.zero }
        _G._VelHijackStore = HIJACK
    end
    HIJACK.v = Vector3.zero

    local LP = LocalPlayer

    if not _G._VelHijackInstalled then
        local ok = pcall(function()
            local _mt = getrawmetatable(game)
            setreadonly(_mt, false)

            local _oi = _mt.__index
            local _on = _mt.__newindex

            local function isOurRoot(t, k)
                local _tk = tostring(k)
                if _tk ~= "AssemblyLinearVelocity" and _tk ~= "Velocity" then return false end
                if typeof(t) ~= "Instance" or not t:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
                local _tn = t.Name
                if _tn ~= "HumanoidRootPart" and _tn ~= "Torso" and _tn ~= "UpperTorso" then return false end
                local char = LP.Character
                return char ~= nil and t:IsDescendantOf(char)
            end

            _mt.__index = newcclosure(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(t, k)
                if not checkcaller() then
                    local good, mine = pcall(isOurRoot, t, k)
                    if good and mine then return HIJACK.v end
                end
                return _oi(t, k)
            end))

            _mt.__newindex = newcclosure(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(t, k, v)
                if not checkcaller() then
                    local good, mine = pcall(isOurRoot, t, k)
                    if good and mine then
                        HIJACK.v = v
                        return
                    end
                end
                return _on(t, k, v)
            end))

            setreadonly(_mt, true)
        end)
        _G._VelHijackInstalled = ok
    end

    local _r = Random.new()
    local _acc = 0

    local function _gc()
        local c = LP.Character
        if not c then return nil, nil, nil end
        local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local r = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if h and h.Health > 0 and r then return c, h, r end
        return nil, nil, nil
    end

    RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(function(dt)
        _acc = _acc + dt
        if _acc < 0.016 then return end
        _acc = 0

        local c, h, r = _gc()
        if not c or not h or not r then return end

        local st = h:GetState()
        if h.PlatformStand
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
            HIJACK.v = Vector3.new(0, r.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, 0)
            return
        end

        local m = h.MoveDirection
        if m.Magnitude > 0.05 then
            pcall(function()
                if r.SetNetworkOwner then r:SetNetworkOwner(LP) end
            end)

            local u    = m.Unit
            local fake = tonumber(_G._VioletteccFakeSpeed) or 16
            local real = math.clamp(tonumber(_G._VioletteccGetSpeed and _G._VioletteccGetSpeed()) or 0, 0, 10000)
            local jx   = _r:NextNumber(-0.003, 0.003)
            local jz   = _r:NextNumber(-0.003, 0.003)
            local y    = r.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y

            HIJACK.v = Vector3.new(u.X * fake + jx, y, u.Z * fake + jz)
            r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(u.X * real + jx, y, u.Z * real + jz)
            _G._RaVeLiveSpeed = { t = os.clock(), v = real }
        else
            HIJACK.v = Vector3.new(0, r.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, 0)
        end
    end)
end)()

;(function()

    local LP = LocalPlayer

    local _conns = {}
    local function hubConnect(signal, handler)
        local c = signal:Connect(handler)
        _conns[#_conns + 1] = c
        return c
    end

    local infJumpEnabled = false

    local CFG = {
        jumpPower     = 37,
        maxJumpHeight = 55,
        roofClearance = 4.2,
    }

    local JUMP_COOLDOWN     = 0.08
    local INF_JUMP_PULSE    = 0.06
    local HOLD_JUMP_REFRESH = 0.12

    local Controller = {}
    if _G.VX7InfinityJump and _G.VX7InfinityJump.Destroy then pcall(_G.VX7InfinityJump.Destroy) end
    Controller.Enabled = false

    local _groundY         = nil
    local _roofBlockUntil  = 0
    local _lastJumpTime    = 0
    local _jumpWasGrounded = true
    local _holdStall       = 0
    local _padJumpHeld     = false
    local _hic             = {}
    local _pulseTokens     = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})

    local function holdModeOn()
        return _G._VezyJumpMode == "Hold" or _G._AdaptInfJumpMode == "HOLD"
    end

    local function isAliveRoot(root)
        if not (root and root.Parent) then return false end
        local char = root.Parent
        if char ~= LP.Character then return false end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        return hum ~= nil and hum.Health > 0
    end

    local function stopNamed(root, prefix)
        if not root then return end
        local lv = root:FindFirstChild(prefix .. "LinearVelocity")
        if lv and lv:IsA("LinearVelocity") then
            _pulseTokens[lv] = nil
            pcall(function() lv.Enabled = false; lv.LineVelocity = 0; lv:Destroy() end)
        end
        local att = root:FindFirstChild(prefix .. "Attachment")
        if att and att:IsA("Attachment") then pcall(function() att:Destroy() end) end
    end

    local function getVertical(root, prefix)
        if not isAliveRoot(root) then return nil end
        local attName, lvName = prefix .. "Attachment", prefix .. "LinearVelocity"
        local att = root:FindFirstChild(attName)
        if not (att and att:IsA("Attachment")) then
            att = Instance.new("Attachment"); att.Name = attName; att.Parent = root
        end
        local lv = root:FindFirstChild(lvName)
        if not (lv and lv:IsA("LinearVelocity")) then
            lv = Instance.new("LinearVelocity"); lv.Name = lvName; lv.Parent = root
        end
        lv.Attachment0 = att
        lv.RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
        lv.VelocityConstraintMode = Enum.VelocityConstraintMode.Line
        lv.LineDirection = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
        pcall(function() lv.ForceLimitsEnabled = false end)
        return lv
    end

    local function pulseVertical(root, prefix, speed, duration)
        local lv = getVertical(root, prefix)
        if not lv then return nil end
        local token = {}
        _pulseTokens[lv] = token
        lv.LineVelocity = speed or 0
        lv.Enabled = true
        task.delay(duration or 0.1, function()
            if _pulseTokens[lv] ~= token then return end
            _pulseTokens[lv] = nil
            if lv.Parent then stopNamed(root, prefix) end
        end)
        return lv
    end

    local function readRootVelocity(root)
        if not (root and root.Parent) then return Vector3.zero end
        local ok, v = pcall(function() return root:GetVelocityAtPosition(root.Position) end)
        return ok and v or Vector3.zero
    end

    local _roofParams = RaycastParams.new()
    _roofParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    pcall(function() _roofParams.RespectCanCollide = true end)

    local function checkRoofCollision(root, dt)
        local char = LP.Character
        if not (char and root and root.Parent) then return false end

        _roofParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { char }

        local upwardSpeed = math.max(readRootVelocity(root).Y, 0)
        if upwardSpeed <= 0.25 then return false end

        local frameTime = math.clamp(dt or (1 / 60), 1 / 240, 1 / 15)
        local roofDistance = (CFG.roofClearance or 4.2) + math.min(upwardSpeed * frameTime, 0.9)

        local roofHit = workspace:Raycast(root.Position, Vector3.new(0, roofDistance, 0), _roofParams)
        if not roofHit then return false end

        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then hum.Jump = false end

        local roofVel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local infJumpActive = root:FindFirstChild("InfJumpLinearVelocity") ~= nil
        if roofVel.Y > 0 and (infJumpActive or roofVel.Y > 60) then
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(roofVel.X, 0, roofVel.Z)
        end

        _roofBlockUntil = tick() + 0.12
        return true
    end

    local function jumpPower()
        return math.clamp(tonumber(CFG.jumpPower) or 37, 35, 48)
    end

    local function jumpCapped(root)
        if not root or not _groundY then return false end
        return (root.Position.Y - _groundY) >= (tonumber(CFG.maxJumpHeight) or 55)
    end

    local function tryPulse(root, hum, manualRequest)
        if not (root and hum) or not isAliveRoot(root) then return false end
        local now = tick()
        if now < _roofBlockUntil then return false end
        if jumpCapped(root) then
            if root:FindFirstChild("InfJumpLinearVelocity") then stopNamed(root, "InfJump") end
            return false
        end
        if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
            _jumpWasGrounded = true; _lastJumpTime = 0; _groundY = root.Position.Y
            return false
        end
        if _jumpWasGrounded then _jumpWasGrounded = false; return false end
        if now - _lastJumpTime < JUMP_COOLDOWN then return false end
        if not manualRequest and readRootVelocity(root).Y > 32 then return false end
        _lastJumpTime = now
        pulseVertical(root, "InfJump", jumpPower(), INF_JUMP_PULSE)
        return true
    end

    local function isGamepad(input)
        return input.UserInputType.Name:sub(1, 7) == "Gamepad"
    end

    local function startHoldInfJump()
        if _hic.loop then return end

        _hic.padDown = hubConnect(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input)
            if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA and isGamepad(input) then
                _padJumpHeld = true
            end
        end)
        _hic.padUp = hubConnect(UserInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
            if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA and isGamepad(input) then
                _padJumpHeld = false
            end
        end)

        _hic.jumpReq = hubConnect(UserInputService.JumpRequest, function()
            if not infJumpEnabled then return end
            local char = LP.Character; if not char then return end
            local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum  = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not (root and hum) then return end
            if not holdModeOn() then
                -- Manual mode mirrors the reference behavior: every jump
                -- request preserves horizontal movement and gives a 50 Y lift.
                local velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(velocity.X, 50, velocity.Z)
            end
        end)

        _hic.loop = hubConnect(RunService.Heartbeat, LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(dt)
            if not infJumpEnabled then return end
            local char = LP.Character; if not char then return end
            local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); if not root then return end
            local hum  = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid");   if not hum then return end

            -- Hold mode matches the reference loop exactly: while Space is
            -- down (or the humanoid is requesting a jump), lift at 55 and
            -- cap extreme downward velocity at -120.
            if holdModeOn() then
                local held = UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) or _padJumpHeld or hum.Jump == true
                local velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                if held and velocity.Y < 35 then
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(velocity.X, 55, velocity.Z)
                elseif velocity.Y < -120 then
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(velocity.X, -120, velocity.Z)
                end
                return
            end

            -- Manual mode is handled exclusively by JumpRequest above.
        end))
    end

    local function stopHoldInfJump()
        for _, key in ipairs({"jumpReq", "loop", "padDown", "padUp"}) do
            local connection = _hic[key]
            if connection then pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end) end
        end
        local char = LP.Character
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root then stopNamed(root, "InfJump") end
        _hic = {}
        _lastJumpTime = 0
        _jumpWasGrounded = true
        _holdStall = 0
        _roofBlockUntil = 0
        _padJumpHeld = false
    end

    hubConnect(LP.CharacterAdded, function()
        _groundY = nil; _jumpWasGrounded = true
        _lastJumpTime = 0; _holdStall = 0; _roofBlockUntil = 0
        _padJumpHeld = false
    end)

    local function setInfJumpEnabled(on)
        infJumpEnabled = on and true or false
        Controller.Enabled = infJumpEnabled
        Enabled.InfiniteJump = infJumpEnabled
        if infJumpEnabled then
            startHoldInfJump()
        else
            stopHoldInfJump()
        end
    end

    function Controller.SetEnabled(enabled)
        setInfJumpEnabled(enabled == true)
        return Controller.Enabled
    end
    function Controller.GetEnabled()
        return Controller.Enabled == true
    end
    function Controller.Toggle()
        Controller.SetEnabled(not Controller.Enabled)
        return Controller.Enabled
    end
    function Controller.ClearVelocity()
        local char = LP.Character
        stopNamed(char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), "InfJump")
    end
    function Controller.Destroy()
        setInfJumpEnabled(false)
        for _, c in ipairs(_conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
        _conns = {}
        if _G.VX7InfinityJump == Controller then _G.VX7InfinityJump = nil end
        if _G._CandyInfinityJump == Controller then _G._CandyInfinityJump = nil end
        if _G.HoldInfJump and _G.HoldInfJump.cfg == CFG then _G.HoldInfJump = nil end
    end

    _G.VX7InfinityJump    = Controller
    _G._CandyInfinityJump = Controller
    _G.HoldInfJump = {
        cfg   = CFG,
        start = startHoldInfJump,
        stop  = stopHoldInfJump,
    }

    startInfiniteJump     = function() setInfJumpEnabled(true)  end
    stopInfiniteJump      = function() setInfJumpEnabled(false) end
    _G.VezyEnableInfJump  = startInfiniteJump
    _G.VezyDisableInfJump = stopInfiniteJump

    if _G._VezyJumpMode == "Single" then _G._VezyJumpMode = "Manual" end
    _G._VezyJumpMode      = _G._VezyJumpMode or "Manual"
    _G._AdaptInfJumpMode  = _G._AdaptInfJumpMode or "MANUAL"

    setInfJumpEnabled(Enabled.InfiniteJump == true)
end)()

;(function()

    local LP = LocalPlayer

    if _G._VioletteccNoPlayerCollision and _G._VioletteccNoPlayerCollision.Destroy then
        pcall(_G._VioletteccNoPlayerCollision.Destroy)
    end

    local _wfConns = {}
    local function track(c)
        _wfConns[#_wfConns + 1] = c
        return c
    end

    local function clearCharacter(char)
        if not char then return end
        for _, part in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.CanCollide then
                part.CanCollide = false
            end
        end
    end

    local function disableOtherCollisions()
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
                clearCharacter(plr.Character)
            end
        end
    end

    local function watchCharacter(char)
        task.spawn(function()
            for _ = 1, 12 do
                if not char.Parent then return end
                clearCharacter(char)
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function watchPlayer(plr)
        if plr == LP then return end
        if plr.Character then watchCharacter(plr.Character) end
        track(plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(watchCharacter))
    end

    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        watchPlayer(plr)
    end
    track(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(watchPlayer))

    local _acc = 0
    track(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(dt)
        _acc = _acc + dt
        if _acc < 0.2 then return end
        _acc = 0
        pcall(disableOtherCollisions)
    end)))

    _G._VioletteccNoPlayerCollision = {
        Refresh = disableOtherCollisions,
        Destroy = function()
            for _, c in ipairs(_wfConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            _wfConns = {}
            if _G._VioletteccNoPlayerCollision then _G._VioletteccNoPlayerCollision = nil end
        end,
    }
end)()

;(function()

    local LP = LocalPlayer

    if _G._VioletteccAutoSpeedRuntime and _G._VioletteccAutoSpeedRuntime.Destroy then
        pcall(_G._VioletteccAutoSpeedRuntime.Destroy)
    end

    local _conns = {}
    local function hubConnect(signal, handler)
        local c = signal:Connect(handler)
        _conns[#_conns + 1] = c
        return c
    end

    _G._VynxAutoSpeedRange = tonumber(_G._VynxAutoSpeedRange) or 15

    local RT = { active = false, previous = nil, graceUntil = 0, conn = nil }

    local function paintCarryModes()
        pcall(Spd.Refresh)
        if _G._VioletteccSyncMobileSpeedBtns then pcall(_G._VioletteccSyncMobileSpeedBtns) end
    end

    local function isIgnoredCarryTool(name)
        local n = tostring(name or ""):lower()
        return n:find("bat") or n:find("slap") or n:find("medusa")
            or n:find("head") or n:find("stone")
    end

    local function isCarryingBrainrot(char)
        if not char then return false end
        if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true or LP:GetAttribute("AntiKick") == true
            or char:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then return true end
        for _, name in ipairs({"Carrying", "IsCarrying", "Grabbed", "Holding", "StealHold", "HasGrab"}) do
            local value = char:FindFirstChild(name, true)
            if value and ((value:IsA("BoolValue") and value.Value)
                or (value:IsA("ObjectValue") and value.Value)
                or (value:IsA("StringValue") and value.Value ~= "")) then return true end
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            local n = child.Name:lower()
            if child:IsA("Tool") and not isIgnoredCarryTool(n) then return true end
            if child:IsA("Model") and child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                and (n:find("brainrot") or n:find("animal") or n:find("carry")
                    or n:find("grab") or n:find("steal") or n:find("hold")) then return true end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function enableAutoCarrySpeed()
        if RT.active then RT.graceUntil = tick() + 0.75; return end
        RT.previous = { family = Spd.family, carry = Spd.carry }
        RT.active = true
        RT.graceUntil = tick() + 0.75
        Spd.carry = true
        paintCarryModes()
    end

    local function disableAutoCarrySpeed()
        if not RT.active then return end
        local previous = RT.previous
        RT.active, RT.previous = false, nil
        if previous then Spd.family, Spd.carry = previous.family, previous.carry end
        paintCarryModes()
    end

    local softSteal = { spots = {}, nextScan = 0, latched = false, held = false, armed = true }

    local function softStealScan()
        local spots = {}
        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then softSteal.spots = spots; return end
        for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
            local yourBase = sign and sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
            local mine = yourBase and yourBase:IsA("BillboardGui") and yourBase.Enabled == true
            if not mine then
                local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
                if podiums then
                    for _, podium in ipairs(podiums:GetChildren()) do
                        local base = podium:FindFirstChild("Base")
                        local spawnPart = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                        if spawnPart then spots[#spots + 1] = spawnPart.Position end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        softSteal.spots = spots
    end

    local function softStealApply()
        if not RT.active then
            RT.previous = { family = Spd.family, carry = Spd.carry }
            RT.active = true
        end
        RT.graceUntil = tick() + 0.75
        Spd.carry = true
        paintCarryModes()
    end

    local function softStealInRange(char)
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then return false end
        local range = tonumber(_G._VynxAutoSpeedRange) or 15
        local target = _G._VioletteccStealTargetPos or _G._RaVeStealTargetPos
        if typeof(target) == "Vector3" and (root.Position - target).Magnitude <= range then
            return true
        end
        local now = tick()
        if now >= softSteal.nextScan then
            softSteal.nextScan = now + 0.5
            softStealScan()
        end
        for _, spot in ipairs(softSteal.spots) do
            if (root.Position - spot).Magnitude <= range then return true end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function startAutoSpeed()
        if RT.conn then RT.conn:Disconnect() end
        RT.conn = hubConnect(RunService.RenderStepped, LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            if not _G._VynxAutoSpeedOn then disableAutoCarrySpeed(); return end
            local char = LP.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not char or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then disableAutoCarrySpeed(); return end

            if _G._VynxAutoSpeedMode == "Soft Steal" then
                local inRange = softStealInRange(char)
                local carrying = isCarryingBrainrot(char)
                if not inRange then softSteal.armed = true end
                if not inRange or Spd.carry ~= true then softSteal.latched = false end
                if inRange and softSteal.armed and not softSteal.latched then
                    softSteal.latched = true
                    softSteal.held = false
                    softStealApply()
                end
                if RT.active then
                    if carrying then
                        softSteal.held = true
                        RT.graceUntil = tick() + 0.75
                    elseif softSteal.held then
                        softSteal.held = false
                        softSteal.latched = false
                        softSteal.armed = false
                        disableAutoCarrySpeed()
                    elseif not inRange and tick() > RT.graceUntil then
                        softSteal.latched = false
                        disableAutoCarrySpeed()
                    end
                end
                return
            end

            if isCarryingBrainrot(char) then
                enableAutoCarrySpeed()
            elseif RT.active then
                disableAutoCarrySpeed()
            end
        end))
    end

    local function stopAutoSpeed()
        if RT.conn then RT.conn:Disconnect(); RT.conn = nil end
        softSteal.latched, softSteal.held, softSteal.armed = false, false, true
        disableAutoCarrySpeed()
    end

    hubConnect(LP.CharacterAdded, function()
        softSteal.latched, softSteal.held, softSteal.armed = false, false, true
    end)

    _G._VioletteccStartAutoSpeed = startAutoSpeed
    _G._VioletteccStopAutoSpeed  = stopAutoSpeed

    _G._AdaptStartAutoCarry = startAutoSpeed
    _G._AdaptStopAutoCarry  = stopAutoSpeed
    _G._CandyIsCarrying     = isCarryingBrainrot
    _G.AutoCarrySpeed = {
        IsCarryingBrainrot = isCarryingBrainrot,
        Enable  = enableAutoCarrySpeed,
        Disable = disableAutoCarrySpeed,
    }

    _G._VioletteccAutoSpeedRuntime = {
        Destroy = function()
            stopAutoSpeed()
            for _, c in ipairs(_conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            _conns = {}
            _G._VioletteccAutoSpeedRuntime = nil
        end,
    }

    startAutoSpeed()
end)()

;(function()

    local LP = LocalPlayer

    if _G._VioletteccAntiRagdollRuntime and _G._VioletteccAntiRagdollRuntime.Destroy then
        pcall(_G._VioletteccAntiRagdollRuntime.Destroy)
    end

    -- Anti Ragdoll ported from Bubble Hub: recover from Physics/Ragdoll/FallingDown
    local AntiRagdoll = {
        Enabled = false,
        Connection = nil,
        ResetCooldown = 0,
    }

    local function arIsRagdolled()
        local char = LP.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return false end
        local state = hum:GetState()
        return state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
    end

    local function arStart()
        if AntiRagdoll.Connection then return end
        AntiRagdoll.Enabled = true
        AntiRagdoll.Connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            if not Enabled.AntiRagdoll then return end
            local char = LP.Character
            if not char then return end
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hum or not root or hum.Health <= 0 then return end

            -- Don't fight an active drop
            if _G.dropActive then return end

            local state, now = hum:GetState(), tick()
            if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
                or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
                or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
                if now - AntiRagdoll.ResetCooldown > 0.15 then
                    AntiRagdoll.ResetCooldown = now
                    pcall(function()
                        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                        root.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                        root.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                            if obj:IsA("Motor6D") then obj.Enabled = true end
                            if obj:IsA("Constraint") then obj.Enabled = true end
                        end
                        workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum
                        local PM = LP.PlayerScripts:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
                        if PM then
                            local CM = require(PM:FindFirstChild("ControlModule"))
                            if CM then CM:Enable() end
                        end
                        hum.AutoRotate = true
                        hum.PlatformStand = false
                        hum.Sit = false
                    end)
                end
            end
        end))
    end

    local function arStop()
        AntiRagdoll.Enabled = false
        if AntiRagdoll.Connection then
            AntiRagdoll.Connection:Disconnect()
            AntiRagdoll.Connection = nil
        end
        AntiRagdoll.ResetCooldown = 0
    end

    local _rebindConn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(0.5)
        if not Enabled.AntiRagdoll then return end
        if AntiRagdoll.Connection then
            AntiRagdoll.Connection:Disconnect()
            AntiRagdoll.Connection = nil
        end
        arStart()
    end)

    startAntiRagdoll = arStart
    stopAntiRagdoll  = arStop
    _G._VioletteccStartAntiRagdoll = arStart
    _G._VioletteccStopAntiRagdoll  = arStop

    _G._VioletteccAntiRagdollRuntime = {
        IsRagdolled = arIsRagdolled,
        Destroy = function()
            arStop()
            pcall(function() _rebindConn:Disconnect() end)
            _G._VioletteccAntiRagdollRuntime = nil
        end,
    }

    if Enabled.AntiRagdoll then arStart() end
end)()

;(function()

    local LP = LocalPlayer

    if _G._VioletteccAutoStealRuntime and _G._VioletteccAutoStealRuntime.Destroy then
        pcall(_G._VioletteccAutoStealRuntime.Destroy)
    end

    local _conns = {}
    local function hubConnect(signal, handler)
        local c = signal:Connect(handler)
        _conns[#_conns + 1] = c
        return c
    end

    _G._KawatanStealRadii = _G._KawatanStealRadii or {}
    local StealRadii = _G._KawatanStealRadii
    StealRadii.Normal = tonumber(_G._VynxStealRadiusNormal) or tonumber(Settings.StealRadius) or 62
    StealRadii.Semi   = tonumber(_G._VynxStealRadiusSemi) or 8.7

    local function stealMode()
        return "Normal"
    end
    local function autoStealOn()
        return Enabled.AutoSteal == true
    end
    local function stealDuration()
        return tonumber(Settings.StealDuration) or STEAL_DURATION_FIXED
    end

    _G.K7NormalSteal = _G.K7NormalSteal or {}
    local NormalSteal = _G.K7NormalSteal
    NormalSteal.enabled = false
    NormalSteal.radius = StealRadii.Normal
    NormalSteal.duration = STEAL_DURATION_FIXED
    NormalSteal.delayRadius = tonumber(_G._VynxStealDelayRadius) or tonumber(NormalSteal.delayRadius) or 9
    do
        local pause = tonumber(_G._VynxAutoGrabPause) or 75
        local dur = tonumber(STEAL_DURATION_FIXED) or 1.30
        NormalSteal.stopTime = tonumber(NormalSteal.stopTime) or (dur * (pause / 100))
    end
    NormalSteal.stopEnabled = (NormalSteal.stopEnabled ~= false)
    NormalSteal.data = NormalSteal.data or {}
    NormalSteal.isStealing = false
    NormalSteal.stealConn = nil

    local _normalPlotsCache = nil
    local _normalPlotsCacheTime = 0

    local function _normalGetPlotsRoot()
        local now = tick()
        if _normalPlotsCache and now - _normalPlotsCacheTime < 2 and _normalPlotsCache.Parent then
            return _normalPlotsCache
        end
        _normalPlotsCache = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        _normalPlotsCacheTime = now
        return _normalPlotsCache
    end

    local function _normalIsMyBase(plotName)
        local plotsRoot = _normalGetPlotsRoot()
        if not plotsRoot then return false end
        local plot = plotsRoot:FindFirstChild(plotName)
        if not plot then return false end
        local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
        if sign then
            local yourBase = sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
            if yourBase and yourBase:IsA("BillboardGui") then
                return yourBase.Enabled == true
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function _normalFindNearestPrompt()
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then return nil, nil end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then return nil, nil end
        local plotsRoot = _normalGetPlotsRoot()
        if not plotsRoot then return nil, nil end
        local A = _G.K7NormalSteal
        local radius = tonumber(A.radius) or StealRadii.Normal or 62
        local nearestPrompt, nearestDist, nearestName = nil, math.huge, nil
        local rpos = root.Position
        for _, plot in ipairs(plotsRoot:GetChildren()) do
            if _normalIsMyBase(plot.Name) then continue end
            local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            if not podiums then continue end
            for _, podium in ipairs(podiums:GetChildren()) do
                pcall(function()
                    local base = podium:FindFirstChild("Base")
                    local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                    if spawn then
                        local sp = spawn.Position
                        local dx = sp.X - rpos.X
                        local dy = sp.Y - rpos.Y
                        local dz = sp.Z - rpos.Z
                        local dist = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz)
                        if dist < nearestDist and dist <= radius then
                            local attach = spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
                            if attach then
                                for _, child in ipairs(attach:GetChildren()) do
                                    if child:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and child.ActionText and child.ActionText:find("Steal") then
                                        nearestPrompt = child
                                        nearestDist = dist
                                        nearestName = podium.Name
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        return nearestPrompt, nearestName
    end

    local function _normalResetBar()
        pcall(function()
            if _G.StealBar then _G.StealBar.Reset() end
        end)
    end

    local function _normalSetProgress(progress)
        pcall(function()
            if _G.StealBar then
                _G.StealBar.SetState("STEALING")
                _G.StealBar.SetProgress(math.clamp(tonumber(progress) or 0, 0, 1))
            end
        end)
    end

    local function _normalExecuteSteal(prompt, podName)
        local A = _G.K7NormalSteal
        if A.isStealing or not prompt then return end

        if math.random(30) == 1 then
            for p in pairs(A.data) do
                if not p.Parent then A.data[p] = nil end
            end
        end

        if not A.data[prompt] then
            A.data[prompt] = { hold = {}, trigger = {}, ready = true }
            pcall(function()
                if getconnections then
                    for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan)) do
                        if c.Function then table.insert(A.data[prompt].hold, c.Function) end
                    end
                    for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.Triggered)) do
                        if c.Function then table.insert(A.data[prompt].trigger, c.Function) end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        local data = A.data[prompt]
        if not data.ready then return end
        data.ready = false
        A.isStealing = true

        task.spawn(function()
            for _, f in ipairs(data.hold) do task.spawn(f) end

            local startTime = tick()
            local duration = tonumber(A.duration) or stealDuration()
            local promptFired = false

            if A.stopEnabled then
                while A.isStealing and A.enabled and stealMode() == "Normal" do
                    local elapsed = tick() - startTime
                    if elapsed >= (tonumber(A.stopTime) or 0.96) then break end
                    _normalSetProgress(math.clamp(elapsed / duration, 0, 1))
                    if not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Parent.Parent then break end
                    local char = LP.Character
                    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if hrp and (hrp.Position - prompt.Parent.Parent.Position).Magnitude > (tonumber(A.radius) or 62) then
                        break
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end

                local stopTime = tonumber(A.stopTime) or 0.96
                local stopProgress = math.clamp(stopTime / duration, 0, 1)
                _normalSetProgress(stopProgress)

                local phase2Timeout = math.max(2.99 - stopTime - math.max(duration - stopTime, 0), 0.05)
                local phase2Start = tick()

                while A.isStealing and A.enabled and stealMode() == "Normal" do
                    if tick() - phase2Start >= phase2Timeout then
                        _normalResetBar()
                        data.ready = true
                        A.isStealing = false
                        task.wait()
                        local newPrompt, newName = _normalFindNearestPrompt()
                        if newPrompt then _normalExecuteSteal(newPrompt, newName) end
                        return
                    end
                    if not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Parent.Parent then
                        A.isStealing = false
                        _normalResetBar()
                        data.ready = true
                        return
                    end
                    local char = LP.Character
                    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if hrp then
                        local dist = (hrp.Position - prompt.Parent.Parent.Position).Magnitude
                        if dist <= (tonumber(_G._VynxStealDelayRadius) or tonumber(A.delayRadius) or 9) then
                            break
                        elseif dist > (tonumber(A.radius) or 62) then
                            A.isStealing = false
                            _normalResetBar()
                            data.ready = true
                            return
                        end
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end

                if A.isStealing and A.enabled and stealMode() == "Normal" then
                    local fillStart = tick()
                    local fillDuration = math.max(duration - stopTime, 0.05)
                    while true do
